### [71] [Identifying Privacy Concerns in Upcoming Software Release: A Peek into the Future](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01393)
*Aurek Chattopadhyay,Nan Niu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Pre-PI是一种在软件版本发布前预测隐私问题的方法，通过将候选功能与现有隐私评论进行语义映射，模拟用户反馈并生成隐私问题摘要，优于依赖发布后反馈的Hark方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前软件发布前缺乏隐私问题识别工具，用户评论仅在发布后才可用，导致开发者无法在发布前主动缓解隐私问题。

Method: 首先将现有功能映射到语义相似的隐私评论以学习功能-隐私评论关系，然后模拟候选功能的反馈并生成简洁的隐私问题摘要。在三个真实应用上评估。

Result: Pre-PI能够生成额外的有效隐私问题，并比Hark更早识别这些隐私问题，使开发者能够在发布前主动缓解。

Conclusion: Pre-PI为发布前隐私问题预测提供了可行的解决方案，可有效支持发布经理和开发者在软件发布前识别和缓解隐私风险。

Abstract: Identifying the features to be released in the next version of software, from a pool of potential candidates, is a challenging problem. User feedback from app stores is frequently used by software vendors for the evolution of apps across releases. Privacy feedback, although smaller in volume, carries a larger impact influencing app's success. Multiple existing work has focused on summarizing privacy concerns at the app level and has also shown that developers utilize feedback to implement security and privacy-related changes in subsequent releases. However, the current literature offers little support for release managers and developers in identifying privacy concerns prior to release. This gap exists as user reviews are typically available in app stores only after new features of a software system is released. In this paper, we introduce Pre-PI, a novel approach that summarizes privacy concerns for to-be-released features. Our method first maps existing features to semantically similar privacy reviews to learn feature-privacy review relations. We then simulate feedback for candidate features and generate concise summaries of privacy concerns. We evaluate Pre-PI across three real-world apps, and compare it with Hark, a state-of-the-art method that relies on post-release user feedback to identify privacy concerns. Results show that Pre-PI generates additional valid privacy concerns and identifies these concerns earlier than Hark, allowing proactive mitigation prior to release.

</details>


### [72] [AI Engineering Blueprint for On-Premises Retrieval-Augmented Generation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01395)
*Nicolas Weeger,Jakob Winkler,Annika Stiehl,Jóakim von Kistowski,Christian Uhl,Stefan Geißelsöder*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对企业数据保护法规限制云服务使用的问题，提出了一套面向本地化部署的可扩展企业级RAG（检索增强生成）系统AI工程蓝图，包含端到端参考架构、参考应用及最佳实践，并已在GitHub开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业RAG系统虽日益普及，但严格的数据保护法规限制了云服务的使用，导致许多组织需要本地化部署。现有的蓝图和参考架构主要面向云端部署且缺乏企业级组件，尚未有全面的本地化实施框架来满足这一需求。

Method: 该蓝图基于4+1视图模型描述端到端参考架构，开发了适用于本地化部署的参考应用程序，并制定了工具、开发及CI/CD流水线的最佳实践。通过与行业合作伙伴的案例研究和专家访谈来评估其实际效果。

Result: 产出了一套完整的企业级RAG本地化部署蓝图，包含参考架构、参考应用和最佳实践，所有内容已在GitHub上公开，将通过实践验证其可行性和效益。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了企业RAG本地化部署领域的空白，为企业将RAG集成到现有基础设施提供了系统性指导框架。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems are gaining traction in enterprise settings, yet stringent data protection regulations prevent many organizations from using cloud-based services, necessitating on-premises deployments. While existing blueprints and reference architectures focus on cloud deployments and lack enterprise-grade components, comprehensive on-premises implementation frameworks remain scarce.
  This paper aims to address this gap by presenting a comprehensive AI engineering blueprint for scalable on-premises enterprise RAG solutions. It is designed to address common challenges and streamline the integration of RAG into existing enterprise infrastructure. The blueprint provides: (1) an end-to-end reference architecture described using the 4+1 view model, (2) a reference application for on-premises deployment, and (3) best practices for tooling, development, and CI/CD pipelines, all publicly available on GitHub. Ongoing case studies and expert interviews with industry partners will assess its practical benefits.

</details>


### [73] [Reproducible, Explainable, and Effective Evaluations of Agentic AI for Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01437)
*Jingyue Li,André Storhaug*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是关于Agentic AI在软件工程领域评估实践的位置论文。通过分析18篇顶会论文，识别了当前评估方法的局限性，并提出了一套促进可复现、可解释、有效评估的指南，建议公开TAR轨迹和LLM交互数据以支持跨方法系统比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Agentic AI研究中，LLM作为黑盒模型难以解释其优越性，且评估设计描述的缺失导致结果复现困难，需要系统性地梳理和改善评估实践。

Method: 采用系统性文献分析方法，收集了ICSE 2026、ICSE 2025、FSE 2025、ASE 2025、ISSTA 2025上发表的18篇相关论文进行分析。

Result: 识别了当前Agentic AI评估实践的主流方法和主要局限。

Conclusion: 提出评估指南与建议，推荐公开TAR轨迹和LLM交互数据以实现跨方法系统比较，并通过概念验证案例研究证明了该方案可行性。

Abstract: With the advancement of Agentic AI, researchers are increasingly leveraging autonomous agents to address challenges in software engineering (SE). However, the large language models (LLMs) that underpin these agents often function as black boxes, making it difficult to justify the superiority of Agentic AI approaches over baselines. Furthermore, missing information in the evaluation design description frequently renders the reproduction of results infeasible. To synthesize current evaluation practices for Agentic AI in SE, this study analyzes 18 papers on the topic, published or accepted by ICSE 2026, ICSE 2025, FSE 2025, ASE 2025, and ISSTA 2025. The analysis identifies prevailing approaches and their limitations in evaluating Agentic AI for SE, both in current research and potential future studies. To address these shortcomings, this position paper proposes a set of guidelines and recommendations designed to empower reproducible, explainable, and effective evaluations of Agentic AI in software engineering. In particular, we recommend that Agentic AI researchers make their Thought-Action-Result (TAR) trajectories and LLM interaction data, or summarized versions of these artifacts, publicly accessible. Doing so will enable subsequent studies to more effectively analyze the strengths and weaknesses of different Agentic AI approaches. To demonstrate the feasibility of such comparisons, we present a proof-of-concept case study that illustrates how TAR trajectories can support systematic analysis across approaches.

</details>


### [74] [Fuzzing with Agents? Generators Are All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01442)
*Vasudev Vikram,Rohan Padhye*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Gentoo利用LLM编码智能体自动合成针对特定模糊测试目标的输入生成器，在7个Java库基准测试中，4个取得统计显著更高的分支覆盖率，且不需要覆盖率引导和变异策略，表明AI可自动生成比肩甚至超越人类专家的领域特定生成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的覆盖引导模糊测试依赖轻量级输入生成器与变异的结合；而高度定制化的领域特定生成器虽能深入探索程序状态，但编写和测试成本高昂。研究者希望探索AI是否能自动合成此类目标特定的生成器，从而降低人工成本并提升模糊测试效果。

Method: 研究提出Gentoo系统，由LLM编码智能体驱动，可访问终端和目标代码及库源码，迭代式合成并优化输入生成器，辅以细粒度的谓词级覆盖率反馈。评估了三种配置方案，与人类编写的生成器在7个真实Java库的模糊测试目标上进行对比实验。

Result: 在7个基准测试中，智能体合成的生成器在4个上实现了统计显著更高的分支覆盖率；关键发现是覆盖率引导和变异策略对智能体生成器无统计显著益处，但对所有人写生成器均有显著作用。

Conclusion: 智能体生成的生成器所编码的结构性和语义逻辑使覆盖率引导变得基本不必要，验证了AI编码智能体可自动合成高质量领域特定输入生成器的可行性，为自动化模糊测试生成提供了新方向。

Abstract: Modern generator-based fuzzing techniques combine lightweight input generators with coverage-guided mutation as a method of exploring deep execution paths in a target program. A complimentary approach in prior research focuses on creating highly customized, domain-specific generators that encode structural and semantic logic sufficient enough to reach deep program states; the challenge comes from the overhead of writing and testing these complex generators. We investigate whether AI coding agents can automatically synthesize such target-specific generators, and whether the resulting generators are strong enough to obviate the need for coverage guidance and mutation entirely.
  Our approach, Gentoo, is comprised of an LLM coding agent (provided terminal access and source code of the fuzz target and its library) instructed to iteratively synthesize and refine an input generator, and optionally provided fine-grained predicate-level coverage feedback. We evaluate three configurations of Gentoo against human-written generators on fuzz targets for 7 real-world Java libraries. Our findings show that agent-synthesized generators achieve statistically significantly higher branch coverage than human-written baseline generators on 4 of 7 benchmarks. Critically, the use of coverage guidance and mutation strategies is not statistically significantly beneficial for agent-synthesized generators, but is significant for all human-written generators, suggesting that structural and semantic logic encoded in the agent generators makes coverage guidance largely unnecessary.

</details>


### [75] [MOVis: A Visual Analytics Tool for Surfacing Missed Patches Across Software Variants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01494)
*Jorge Gonzalo Delgado Cervantes,John Businge,Daniel Ogenrwot*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MOVis是一款轻量级交互式桌面工具，可视化软件变体间的"错失机会"(MO)补丁，帮助开发者快速定位缺失修复、理解所需适配、更高效地维护软件变体间的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克隆-拥有( Clone-and-own )开发模式导致软件变体家族独立演化，一个仓库的重要修复往往缺失于其他变体中。PaReco虽已发现大量此类"错失机会"(MO)补丁，但其纯文本输出难以支持开发者理解修复的传播位置和方式。

Method: MOVis加载PaReco的MO分类结果，提供修补后代码块与有bug代码块的并排对比，高亮显示对应区域，暴露阻碍重用的结构差异。

Result: 开发者可快速定位缺失修复、理解所需适配、更高效地在软件变体间保持一致性。

Conclusion: MOVis作为PaReco的补充工具，通过可视化方式提升了MO补丁的可理解性和可操作性。

Abstract: Clone-and-own development produces families of related software variants that evolve independently. As variants diverge, important fixes applied in one repository are often missing in others. PaReco has shown that thousands of such missed opportunity (MO) patches exist across real ecosystems, yet its textual output provides limited support for understanding where and how these fixes should be propagated. We present MOVis, a lightweight, interactive desktop tool that visualizes MO patches between a source and target variant. MOVis loads PaReco's MO classifications and presents patched and buggy hunks side-by-side, highlighting corresponding regions and exposing structural differences that hinder reuse. This design enables developers to quickly locate missed fixes, understand required adaptations, and more efficiently maintain consistency across software variants. The tool, replication package, and demonstration video are available at https://zenodo.org/records/18356553 and https://youtu.be/Ac-gjBxHJ3Y.

</details>


### [76] [From SWE-ZERO to SWE-HERO: Execution-free to Execution-based Fine-tuning for Software Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01496)
*Nikolai Ludwig,Wasi Uddin Ahmad,Somshubra Majumdar,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SWE-ZERO to SWE-HERO是一个两阶段SFT训练方案，通过蒸馏开源前沿模型，在SWE-bench上实现最优结果。方案包含两个阶段：(1) SWE-ZERO使用大规模无需执行的轨迹数据来掌握代码语义和仓库级推理；(2) SWE-HERO使用目标明确、基于执行的 refinement 来将这些语义直觉转化为严格的工程工作流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有方法依赖大量计算资源的问题，提出一种轻量级的蒸馏策略来训练开源模型在软件工程任务上达到前沿性能。

Method: 两阶段训练方案：(1) SWE-ZERO阶段：利用大规模执行-free轨迹数据进行预训练，掌握代码语义和仓库推理能力；(2) SWE-HERO阶段：使用目标性、execution-backed refinement进行精细调整，将语义理解转化为实际工程能力。同时在SWE-bench Multilingual上验证跨语言泛化能力。

Result: SWE-HERO-32B在SWE-bench Verified上达到62.2%的解决率；尽管仅在Python上训练，在SWE-bench Multilingual上仍达到44.1%，证明了方法的跨语言泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过进化式refinement策略和两阶段训练范式，可以有效地从开源大模型中蒸馏出高性能的代码智能体，为资源受限场景提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce SWE-ZERO to SWE-HERO, a two-stage SFT recipe that achieves state-of-the-art results on SWE-bench by distilling open-weight frontier LLMs. Our pipeline replaces resource-heavy dependencies with an evolutionary refinement strategy: (1) SWE-ZERO utilizes large-scale, execution-free trajectories to master code semantics and repository-level reasoning, and (2) SWE-HERO applies targeted, execution-backed refinement to transition these semantic intuitions into rigorous engineering workflows. Our empirical results set a new benchmark for open-source models of comparable size. We release a dataset of 300k SWE-ZERO and 13k SWE-HERO trajectories distilled from Qwen3-Coder-480B, alongside a suite of agents based on the Qwen2.5-Coder series. Notably, SWE-HERO-32B achieves a 62.2% resolution rate on SWE-bench Verified. Furthermore, despite being trained exclusively on Python, our agents demonstrate robust zero-shot transferability on SWE-bench Multilingual, reaching 44.1% and confirming the paradigm's generalizability across diverse languages.

</details>


### [77] [ToolMisuseBench: An Offline Deterministic Benchmark for Tool Misuse and Recovery in Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01508)
*Akshey Sigdel,Rista Baral*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ToolMisuseBench是一个离线确定性基准测试，用于评估工具误用和恢复能力，包含6800个任务，覆盖CRUD、检索、文件和调度环境，具有可重放的故障注入机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前工具使用智能体常因操作原因失败，即使语言理解能力良好。常见失败原因包括：无效参数、接口漂移、恢复能力弱、重试行为低效等。现有的评估方法缺乏对这类操作故障的系统性测试。

Method: 提出ToolMisuseBench基准测试：1）离线确定性设计，支持可重放的故障注入；2）覆盖CRUD、检索、文件和调度四类环境；3）使用明确的步数、调用次数和重试预算；4）多维度评估指标：成功率、无效调用行为、策略违规、恢复质量和预算效率。

Result: 基线实验表明：具备模式感知的方法在特定故障类型上展现出恢复能力提升；但在授权设置和硬故障场景下，整体成功率仍然有限。

Conclusion: 该基准测试为工具误用和恢复研究提供了标准化评估框架；未来改进方向应聚焦于提升模式感知能力和针对复杂故障场景的鲁棒恢复策略。

Abstract: Tool using agents often fail for operational reasons even when language understanding is strong. Common causes include invalid arguments, interface drift, weak recovery, and inefficient retry behavior. We introduce ToolMisuseBench, an offline deterministic benchmark for evaluating tool misuse and recovery under explicit step, call, and retry budgets. The benchmark covers CRUD, retrieval, file, and scheduling environments with replayable fault injection. It reports success, invalid call behavior, policy violations, recovery quality, and budgeted efficiency. We release a public dataset with 6800 tasks and a reproducible evaluation pipeline. Baseline results show fault specific recovery gains for schema aware methods, while overall success remains limited under the released authorization and hard failure settings.

</details>


### [78] [Are Benchmark Tests Strong Enough? Mutation-Guided Diagnosis and Augmentation of Regression Suites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01518)
*Chenglin Li,Yisen Xu,Zehao Wang,Shin Hwei Tan,Tse-Hsun,Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: STING通过语义变体诊断技术增强SWE-bench测试套件，发现77%的实例存在测试漏洞，生成1014个新测试后使顶级修复agent的通过率下降4.2%-9.0%，揭示现有benchmark评估高估了agent的真实能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SWE-bench等测试驱动基准存在固有缺陷：回归测试强度不足时，合乎语法但语义错误的补丁也能通过测试，导致报告的成功率虚高，无法真实反映自动化修复能力。

Method: STING框架的核心思路：(1)对ground-truth补丁进行语义变更生成变体；(2)筛选出仍能通过原有测试的变体——这些变体暴露了测试约束不足的行为缺口；(3)基于缺口生成针对性回归测试；(4)测试须满足三重要求：通过ground-truth补丁、至少在一个存活变体上失败、经行为保持变换验证以防过拟合。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified上，77%的实例存在至少一个存活变体；STING生成1014个有效测试覆盖211个实例；补丁的行覆盖率和分支覆盖率分别提升10.8%和9.5%；重新评估TOP-10修复agent后，其解决率下降4.2%-9.0%。

Conclusion: 可靠的基准评估不仅依赖补丁生成能力，同样依赖测试充分性；当前SWE-bench的成功率存在系统性高估；未来需要更严格的测试套件作为评估标准。

Abstract: Benchmarks driven by test suites, notably SWE-bench, have become the de facto standard for measuring the effectiveness of automated issue-resolution agents: a generated patch is accepted whenever it passes the accompanying regression tests. In practice, however, insufficiently strong test suites can admit plausible yet semantically incorrect patches, inflating reported success rates. We introduce STING, a framework for targeted test augmentation that uses semantically altered program variants as diagnostic stressors to uncover and repair weaknesses in benchmark regression suites. Variants of the ground-truth patch that still pass the existing tests reveal under-constrained behaviors; these gaps then guide the generation of focused regression tests. A generated test is retained only if it (i) passes on the ground-truth patch, (ii) fails on at least one variant that survived the original suite, and (iii) remains valid under behavior-preserving transformations designed to guard against overfitting. Applied to SWE-bench Verified, STING finds that 77% of instances contain at least one surviving variant. STING produces 1,014 validated tests spanning 211 instances and increases patch-region line and branch coverage by 10.8% and 9.5%, respectively. Re-assessing the top-10 repair agents with the strengthened suites lowers their resolved rates by 4.2%-9.0%, revealing that a substantial share of previously passing patches exploit weaknesses in the benchmark tests rather than faithfully implementing the intended fix. These results underscore that reliable benchmark evaluation depends not only on patch generation, but equally on test adequacy.

</details>


### [79] [EpiDroid: Dependency-Guided Recomposition for Deep State Discovery in Mobile GUI Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01522)
*Jiahui Song,Jiaxin Zhi,Kangjia Zhao,Chen Zhi,Junxiao Han,Xinkui Zhao,Nan Wang,Shuiguang Deng,Jianwei Yin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: EpiDroid是一个黑盒、可插拔的框架，通过语义状态依赖感知增强现有移动应用GUI探索器。它将原始轨迹提炼为稳定的测试片段以提取依赖关系，采用重组合-重放范式进行影响推理，并通过迭代反馈逐步完善状态依赖图，从而系统性地访问深层应用状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化GUI探索方法忽视测试片段间的状态依赖关系，导致冗余探索且无法访问深层应用状态。

Method: 1) 将原始轨迹蒸馏为稳定的测试片段以提取底层依赖关系；2) 采用"重组合-重放"范式，通过LLM和确定性重放对高价值可变状态元素进行影响推理；3) 通过迭代反馈完善状态依赖图。

Result: 集成到工业级和研究级工具后在20个真实应用上评估，平均代码覆盖率提升10-28%，相比基线单独运行可获得3-4倍更高的覆盖率增益。

Conclusion: 依赖引导的重组合能够解锁正向探索无法访问的深层状态，且与额外预算无关，证明了状态依赖感知对于深度GUI探索的重要性。

Abstract: The increasing scale and complexity of mobile applications make automated GUI exploration essential for software quality assurance. However, existing methods often neglect state dependencies between test fragments, which leads to redundant exploration and prevents access to deep application states. We introduce EpiDroid, a black-box, pluggable framework that augments existing explorers through semantic state dependency awareness. EpiDroid distills raw traces into stable test fragments to extract underlying dependencies. It then employs a Recomposition-Replay paradigm to perform impact reasoning via LLM and deterministic replay on high-value mutable state elements. Through iterative feedback, EpiDroid refines the state-dependency graph to systematically reach deep application states. We integrated EpiDroid into both industrial and state-of-the-art research tools and evaluated it on 20 real-world apps. The results show that EpiDroid consistently improves the performance of all baselines, increasing average code coverage by 10--28\% and delivering 3--4$\times$ more coverage gain compared to continuing the baselines alone from the same starting point. This demonstrates that dependency-guided recomposition unlocks deep states that forward exploration cannot access, irrespective of additional budget.

</details>


### [80] [ProdCodeBench: A Production-Derived Benchmark for Evaluating AI Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01527)
*Smriti Jha,Matteo Paltenghi,Chandra Maddila,Vijayaraghavan Murali,Shubham Ugare,Satish Chandra*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ProdCodeBench，一个从生产环境真实会话中构建的AI编程智能体评估基准，解决了现有基准与真实使用场景在编程语言分布、提示风格和代码库结构上的差异问题。通过系统分析发现，使用工作验证工具（如测试执行和静态分析）的模型表现更好，表明迭代验证对智能体行为至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试与生产环境中的真实使用场景存在显著差异，包括编程语言分布、提示风格和代码库结构等方面，这使得在工业环境中评估AI编程智能体变得不准确。研究者需要一个能更好反映生产工作负载的基准测试。

Method: 1) 从生产AI编程助手的真实会话中收集数据；2) 使用基于LLM的任务分类；3) 测试相关性验证；4) 多轮运行稳定性检查；5) 样本包含原始提示、提交的代码变更和fail-to-pass测试；6) 覆盖7种编程语言；7) 对四个基础模型进行系统评估。

Result: 模型解决率为53.2%至72.2%；更频繁使用工作验证工具（测试执行、静态分析调用）的模型获得更高解决率。

Conclusion: 迭代验证有助于实现有效的智能体行为；向外部训练的智能体暴露特定代码库的验证机制可能显著提升其在不熟悉环境中的表现；该方法论可供其他组织构建类似的生产驱动基准。

Abstract: Benchmarks that reflect production workloads are better for evaluating AI coding agents in industrial settings, yet existing benchmarks differ from real usage in programming language distribution, prompt style and codebase structure. This paper presents a methodology for curating production-derived benchmarks, illustrated through ProdCodeBench - a benchmark built from real sessions with a production AI coding assistant. We detail our data collection and curation practices including LLM-based task classification, test relevance validation, and multi-run stability checks which address challenges in constructing reliable evaluation signals from monorepo environments. Each curated sample consists of a verbatim prompt, a committed code change and fail-to-pass tests spanning seven programming languages. Our systematic analysis of four foundation models yields solve rates from 53.2% to 72.2% revealing that models making greater use of work validation tools, such as executing tests and invoking static analysis, achieve higher solve rates. This suggests that iterative verification helps achieve effective agent behavior and that exposing codebase-specific verification mechanisms may significantly improve the performance of externally trained agents operating in unfamiliar environments. We share our methodology and lessons learned to enable other organizations to construct similar production-derived benchmarks.

</details>


### [81] [Mitigating Implicit Inconsistencies in Patch Porting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01680)
*Shengyi Pan,Zhongxin Liu,Jiayuan Zhou,Xing Hu,Xin Xia,Shanping Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MIP是一种自动化补丁移植工具，通过协同LLM、编译器和代码分析工具解决补丁从源代码库移植到变体（fork和分支）时的隐式不一致性问题。在跨fork和跨分支两个场景中表现优于最佳基线两倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化补丁移植方法主要处理补丁局部上下文中的不一致，难以解决需要代码库全局映射知识的隐式不一致问题，这类问题对开发者更具挑战性。

Method: MIP采用分层策略：(1)当源标识符存在于目标代码库时，利用编译器诊断信息；(2)否则，从两个代码库中检索匹配代码段作为映射知识，为LLM提供上下文。

Result: 在跨fork和跨分支补丁移植两个代表性场景中，MIP成功解决的补丁数量均达到最佳基线的两倍以上。

Conclusion: 与工业伙伴的用户研究进一步证明了MIP的实用有效性，表明该方法能够有效解决实际开发中的补丁移植问题。

Abstract: Promptly porting patches from a source codebase to its variants (e.g., forks and branches) is essential for mitigating propagated defects and vulnerabilities. Recent studies have explored automated patch porting to reduce manual effort and delay, but existing approaches mainly handle inconsistencies visible in a patch's local context and struggle with those requiring global mapping knowledge between codebases. We refer to such non-local inconsistencies as implicit inconsistencies. Implicit inconsistencies pose greater challenges for developers to resolve due to their non-local nature. To address them, we propose MIP, which enables collaboration among an LLM, a compiler, and code analysis utilities. MIP adopts different strategies for different cases: when source identifiers exist in the target codebase, it leverages compiler diagnostics; otherwise, it retrieves matched code segment pairs from the two codebases as mapping knowledge for mitigation. Experiments on two representative scenarios, cross-fork and cross-branch patch porting, show that MIP successfully resolves more than twice as many patches as the best-performing baseline in both settings. A user study with our industry partner further demonstrates its practical effectiveness.

</details>


### [82] [Fuzzing REST APIs in Industry: Necessary Features and Open Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01759)
*Andrea Arcuri,Alexander Poth,Olsi Rrjolli,Philip Garrett,Juan P. Galeotti*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 大众汽车将学术原型EvoMaster（基于搜索的REST API模糊测试工具）应用于工业环境，分享了在实际部署中获得的教训和改进建议，并对4个API进行了反馈评估，同时对来自4家公司的11名测试专家进行了用户研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行业现有的REST API测试方法耗时且需要专业测试工程师的知识；学术原型工具（如EvoMaster）如何有效集成到工业环境并产生实际影响尚需深入研究。

Method: 2023-2026年在大众汽车使用EvoMaster进行API测试；收集4个API的反馈；开展包含11名测试专家（来自4家公司）的用户研究。

Result: 识别出在工业环境中成功使用EvoMaster所需实现的功能特性；发现多个需要解决的真实世界研究挑战。

Conclusion: 学术原型工具与工业实践之间存在差距，需要进一步研究以弥合这一鸿沟，使自动化测试工具更好地服务于工业环境。

Abstract: REST APIs are widely used in industry, in all different kinds of domains. An example is Volkswagen AG, a German automobile manufacturer. Established testing approaches for REST APIs are time consuming, and require expertise from professional test engineers. Due to its cost and importance, in the scientific literature several approaches have been proposed to automatically test REST APIs. The open-source, search-based fuzzer EvoMaster is one of such tools proposed in the academic literature. However, how academic prototypes can be integrated in industry and have real impact to software engineering practice requires more investigation. In this paper, we report on our experience in using EvoMaster at Volkswagen AG, as an EvoMaster user from 2023 to 2026. We share our learnt lessons, and discuss several features needed to be implemented in EvoMaster to make its use in an industrial context successful. Feedback about value in industrial setups of EvoMaster was given from Volkswagen AG about 4 APIs. Additionally, a user study was conducted involving 11 testing specialists from 4 different companies. We further identify several real-world research challenges that still need to be solved.

</details>


### [83] [TestDecision: Sequential Test Suite Generation via Greedy Optimization and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01799)
*Guoqing Wang,Chengran Yang,Xiaoxuan Zhou,Zeyu Sun,Bo Wang,David Lo,Dan Hao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出TestDecision，利用序列视角和亚模优化理论，将开源LLM转化为神经贪心专家，实现测试用例套件的迭代构建，在分支覆盖率和执行通过率上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源LLM在自动化测试生成中存在"结构短视"问题——无法基于当前覆盖状态生成具有边际增益的新测试用例，这限制了测试套件的质量和效率。

Method: 1) 将测试套件生成形式化为MDP问题，并证明其目标函数具有单调亚模性；2) 将NP难全局优化松弛为可处理的步进贪心策略；3) 提出包含推理框架和RL训练流程的TestDecision，将LLM转化为神经贪心专家。

Result: 在ULT基准上，分支覆盖率提升38.15-52.37%，执行通过率提升298.22-558.88%；7B模型性能可比肩GPT-5.2；发现bug数量比vanilla LLM多58.43-95.45%；在LiveCodeBench上展现优异泛化能力。

Conclusion: TestDecision通过亚模优化理论和RL训练，有效解决了开源LLM在测试套件生成中的结构短视问题，为自动化软件测试提供了高效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: With the rapid evolution of LLMs, automated software testing is witnessing a paradigm shift. While proprietary models like GPT-4o demonstrate impressive capabilities, their high deployment costs and data privacy concerns make open-source LLMs the practical imperative for many academic and industrial scenarios. In the field of automated test generation, it has evolved to iterative workflows to construct test suites based on LLMs. When utilizing open-source LLMs, we empirically observe they lack a suite-level perspective, suffering from structural myopia-failing to generate new tests with large marginal gain based on the current covered status. In this paper, from the perspective of sequences, we formalize test suite generation as a MDP and demonstrate that its objective exhibits monotone submodularity, which enables an effective relaxation of this NP-hard global optimization into a tractable step-wise greedy procedure. Guided by this insight, we propose TestDecision, which transforms LLMs into neural greedy experts. TestDecision consists of two synergistic components: (1) an inference framework which implements test suite construction following a step-wise greedy strategy; and (2) a training pipeline of reinforcement learning which equips the base LLM with sequential test generation ability to maximize marginal gain. Comprehensive evaluations on the ULT benchmark demonstrate that TestDecision significantly outperforms existing advanced methods. It brings an improvement between 38.15-52.37% in branch coverage and 298.22-558.88% in execution pass rate over all base models, achieving a comparable performance on 7B backbone with a much larger proprietary LLM GPT-5.2. Furthermore, TestDecision can find 58.43-95.45% more bugs than vanilla base LLMs and exhibit superior generalization on LiveCodeBench, proving its capability to construct high-quality test suites.

</details>


### [84] [Can Large Language Models Model Programs Formally?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01851)
*Zhiyong Chen,Jialun Cao,Jiarong Wu,Chang Xu,Shing-Chi Cheung*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Model-Bench是一个用于评估大语言模型（LLM）将Python程序建模为形式化验证规范能力的基准测试平台，包含400个Python程序，通过配套模型检查器验证建模质量，实验揭示了现有LLM在此任务上的显著局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型检查（model checking）在形式化验证中相比定理证明（theorem proving）受到的关注较少，主要原因是自动程序建模的困难。为了弥补这一研究空白，需要一个专门评估LLM程序建模能力的基准。

Method: 从三个知名基准（HumanEval、MBPP、LiveCodeBench）提取400个Python程序，构建Model-Bench基准和配套流水线，将LLM建模的程序转换为可验证的模型检查规范，并使用配套模型检查器进行验证。

Result: 实验揭示了当前LLM在程序建模任务上存在显著局限性，为后续研究提供了改进方向。

Conclusion: Model-Bench能够有效评估和改进LLM的程序建模能力，为推动形式化验证领域与LLM的结合提供了重要工具和见解。

Abstract: In the digital age, ensuring the correctness, safety, and reliability of software through formal verification is paramount, particularly as software increasingly underpins critical infrastructure. Formal verification, split into theorem proving and model checking, provides a feasible and reliable path. Unlike theorem proving, which yields notable advances, model checking has been less focused due to the difficulty of automatic program modeling. To fill this gap, we introduce Model-Bench, a benchmark and an accompanying pipeline for evaluating and improving LLMs' program modeling capability by modeling Python programs into verification-ready model checking specifications checkable by its accompanying model checker. Model-Bench comprises 400 Python programs derived from three well-known benchmarks (HumanEval, MBPP, and LiveCodeBench). Our extensive experiments reveal significant limitations in LLMs' program modeling and further provide inspiring directions.

</details>


### [85] [APITestGenie: Generating Web API Tests from Requirements and API Specifications with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02039)
*André Pereira,Bruno Lima,João Pascoal Faria*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: APITestGenie是一款利用大语言模型(LLM)、检索增强生成(RAG)和提示工程从业务需求和OpenAPI规范自动生成API集成测试的工具。在10个真实API(包括来自汽车领域工业合作伙伴的约1000个端点)上评估后，该工具在最多三次尝试内能为89%的业务需求生成语法和语义正确的测试脚本，部分测试还发现了API中先前未知的缺陷，包括端点间的集成问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件系统高度依赖Web API，但创建有意义且可执行的测试脚本仍然是一项大量手动、耗时且容易出错的任务。手动编写API集成测试需要深厚的领域知识，且难以保证测试与业务需求的一致性。

Method: APITestGenie采用三大核心技术：(1) 大语言模型(LLM)用于理解和生成测试逻辑；(2) 检索增强生成(RAG)从OpenAPI规范和相关文档中检索相关信息以增强生成质量；(3) 提示工程(prompt engineering)优化输入以获得更准确的测试脚本输出。

Result: 实验评估了10个真实API(含8个来自汽车领域工业合作伙伴的约1000个端点)：
- 89%的业务需求在最多三次尝试内生成语法和语义有效的测试脚本
- 部分生成的测试发现了API先前未知的缺陷，包括端点间集成问题
- 统计表明API复杂度和业务需求详细程度是影响成功率的主要因素
- API文档详细程度也对结果产生影响

Conclusion: 行业从业者反馈证实该工具具有强烈的采用意愿，能显著减少编写验收测试的手动工作量，并提高测试与业务需求之间的对齐度。该工具成功地将业务需求、OpenAPI规范与可执行测试脚本联系起来，证明了AI辅助测试生成的实用价值。

Abstract: Modern software systems rely heavily on Web APIs, yet creating meaningful and executable test scripts remains a largely manual, time-consuming, and error-prone task. In this paper, we present APITestGenie, a novel tool that leverages Large Language Models (LLMs), Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), and prompt engineering to automatically generate API integration tests directly from business requirements and OpenAPI specifications. We evaluated APITestGenie on 10 real-world APIs, including 8 APIs comprising circa 1,000 live endpoints from an industrial partner in the automotive domain. The tool was able to generate syntactically and semantically valid test scripts for 89\% of the business requirements under test after at most three attempts. Notably, some generated tests revealed previously unknown defects in the APIs, including integration issues between endpoints. Statistical analysis identified API complexity and level of detail in business requirements as primary factors influencing success rates, with the level of detail in API documentation also affecting outcomes. Feedback from industry practitioners confirmed strong interest in adoption, substantially reducing the manual effort in writing acceptance tests, and improving the alignment between tests and business requirements.

</details>


### [86] [A Model-Driven Digital Twin for the Systematic Improvement of DevOps Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02077)
*Achref Samoud,Sara Aissat,Francis Bordeleau*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于BPMN的模型驱动数字孪生框架，用于将CI/CD流水线配置转换为结构化的高级流程表示，支持可视化抽象、故障追踪和版本对比分析，为DevOps流程的监控和演进分析提供模块化可扩展的基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CI/CD流水线的复杂性日益增加，现有工具仅提供执行日志和静态图表示，难以支撑结构化分析、故障诊断和版本演进分析。

Method: 采用BPMN作为建模骨干，通过模型驱动方式将原始CI配置转换为结构化流程表示，构建数字孪生架构。

Result: 实现了流水线结构可视化抽象、故障追踪和系统性版本对比，支持DevOps流程的监控和演进分析。

Conclusion: 基于验证的DT架构原则，提供模块化可扩展的框架，可集成高级分析和优化服务，已通过开源CI/CD项目验证。

Abstract: CI/CD pipelines are central to DevOps practices, yet their growing complexity makes them increasingly difficult to interpret, analyze, and systematically evolve. Existing tooling primarily offers execution logs and static graph representations, providing limited support for structured analysis of pipeline behavior, failures, and version-to-version evolution. This paper presents a model-driven Digital Twin (DT) for CI/CD pipelines that leverages BPMN as a model-ing backbone to transform raw CI configurations into structured, higher-level process representations. The proposed DT architecture enables visual abstraction of pipeline structure, failure tracing, and systematic version comparison, supporting both monitoring and evolution analysis of DevOps processes. Building upon validated DT architectural principles and prior work on build optimization and anomaly detection, the framework provides a modular, extensible foundation for integrating advanced analytical and prescriptive services into software delivery processes. The approach is validated using open-source CI/CD projects, and ongoing work targets the integration of additional improvement services and the extension of the DT to broader DevOps lifecycle processes.

</details>


### [87] [Automated Functional Testing for Malleable Mobile Application Driven from User Intent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02079)
*Yuying Wang,Kaifeng Huang,Hao Deng,Zhiyuan Sun,Jinxuan Zhou,Shengjie Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用户需求驱动的GUI测试生成框架，通过LLM自动实现用户定制功能需求并生成测试用例验证功能正确性，构建了包含6个主流移动应用的基准测试集，证明了端用户驱动的移动应用开发范式的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可配置系统和自适应推荐系统缺乏通用性，无法满足端用户对移动应用个性化定制的需求。核心挑战在于如何自动生成测试用例，可靠地验证用户指定功能的正确性。

Method: 提出一种用户需求驱动的GUI测试生成框架，逐步导航UI、触发目标功能，并构建LLM引导的测试预言机（oracles）进行正确性验证。

Result: 框架能有效验证用户定制功能，在6个流行移动应用上进行了验证，包含正确和故障功能变体。

Conclusion: 证明了从"产品经理驱动"向"终端用户驱动"的移动应用开发范式转移的可行性，为LLM在软件工程中的应用开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Software malleability allows applications to be easily changed, configured, and adapted even after deployment. While prior work has explored configurable systems, adaptive recommender systems, and malleable GUIs, these approaches are often tailored to specific software and lack generalizability. In this work, we envision per-user malleable mobile applications, where end-users can specify requirements that are automatically implemented via LLM-based code generation. However, realizing this vision requires overcoming the key challenge of designing automated test generation that can reliably verify both the presence and correctness of user-specified functionalities. We propose \tool, a user-requirement-driven GUI test generation framework that incrementally navigates the UI, triggers desired functionalities, and constructs LLM-guided oracles to validate correctness. We build a benchmark spanning six popular mobile applications with both correct and faulty user-requested functionalities, demonstrating that \tool effectively validates per-user features and is practical for real-world deployment. Our work highlights the feasibility of shifting mobile app development from a product-manager-driven to an end-user-driven paradigm.

</details>


### [88] [A Case For Host Code Guided GPU Data Race Detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02106)
*Ajay Nayak,Anubhab Ghosh,Arkaprava Basu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: HGRD是一种新型静态分析技术，通过联合分析CPU和GPU代码来检测GPU程序中的数据竞争，同时避免误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU程序中的数据竞争威胁软件可靠性。现有动态技术因需要竞争在测试中显现而遗漏关键竞争；现有静态技术因保守假设变量值而产生大量误报。

Method: HGRD利用一个关键洞察：主机(CPU)代码在启动GPU内核时携带了关于GPU内核参数取值的重要语义信息。通过对CPU和GPU代码进行整体分析，实现精确的数据竞争检测。

Result: 与动态技术iGUARD相比，HGRD捕获更多真阳性竞争；与静态技术GPUVerify和FaialAA相比，HGRD消除所有误报。

Conclusion: HGRD在准确检测真阳性竞争的同时最小化误报，实现了当前最优(state-of-the-art)的数据竞争检测效果。

Abstract: Data races in GPU programs pose a threat to the reliability of GPU-accelerated software stacks. Prior works proposed various dynamic (runtime) and static (compile-time) techniques to detect races in GPU programs. However, dynamic techniques often miss critical races, as they require the races to manifest during testing. While static ones can catch such races, they often generate numerous false alarms by conservatively assuming values of variables/parameters that cannot ever occur during any execution of the program.
  We make a key observation that the host (CPU) code that launches GPU kernels contains crucial semantic information about the values that the GPU kernel's parameters can take during execution. Harnessing this hitherto overlooked information helps accurately detect data races in GPU kernel code. We create HGRD, a new state-of-the-art static analysis technique that performs a holistic analysis of both CPU and GPU code to accurately detect a broad set of true races while minimizing false alarms. While SOTA dynamic techniques, such as iGUARD, miss many true races, HGRD misses none. On the other hand, static techniques such as GPUVerify and FaialAA raise tens of false alarms, where HGRD raises none.

</details>


### [89] [Semantic Evolution over Populations for LLM-Guided Automated Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02134)
*Cuong Chi Le,Minh Le-Anh,Cuong Duc Van,Tien N. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: EvolRepair是一种基于种群的语义进化框架，将LLM驱动的自动程序修复(APR)重新定义为语义进化算法，取代遗传算法的语法操作符，利用结构化执行反馈实现行为一致性分组、修复家族推理和互补修复组合，实验表明显著优于现有LLM-APR方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迭代优化LLM-APR方法面临五大挑战：维持修复假设的多样性、识别语义相关修复家族、组合互补局部修复、利用结构化失败信息、逃脱结构缺陷搜索区域。

Method: 提出EvolRepair框架，将经典遗传算法的搜索范式重新表述用于APR，用LLM驱动的语义感知组件替代语法操作符。核心机制包括：(1)将候选修复组织为行为一致组；(2)利用结构化失败模式引导搜索方向；(3)跨种群重组互补修复洞见生成更强候选。

Result: EvolRepair在修复效果上显著超越现有LLM-APR方法，展示了语义进化范式在程序修复任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于种群的语义进化框架为LLM-APR提供了新范式，通过行为聚类、多样性维持和跨家族重组能力，有效解决了传统迭代优化方法的局限性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently shown strong potential for automated program repair (APR), particularly through iterative refinement that generates and improves candidate patches. However, state-of-the-art iterative refinement LLM-based APR approaches cannot fully address challenges, including maintaining useful diversity among repair hypotheses, identifying semantically related repair families, composing complementary partial fixes, exploiting structured failure information, and escaping structurally flawed search regions. In this paper, we propose a Population-Based Semantic Evolution framework for APR iterative refinement, called EvolRepair, that formulates LLM-based APR as a semantic evolutionary algorithm. EvolRepair reformulates the search paradigm of classic genetic algorithm for APR, but replaces its syntax-based operators with semantics-aware components powered by LLMs and structured execution feedback. Candidate repairs are organized into behaviorally coherent groups, enabling the algorithm to preserve diversity, reason over repair families, and synthesize stronger candidates by recombining complementary repair insights across the population. By leveraging structured failure patterns to guide search direction, EvolRepair can both refine promising repair strategies and shift toward alternative abstractions when necessary. Our experiments show that EvolRepair substantially improves repair effectiveness over existing LLM-based APR approaches.

</details>


### [90] [LLMs as Idiomatic Decompilers: Recovering High-Level Code from x86-64 Assembly for Dart](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02278)
*Raafat Abualazm,Ayman Abo Elhassan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究探索小型专用LLM用于将机器码反编译为Dart和Swift等现代高级语言，发现4B专用模型性能可媲美~480B通用代码模型，并揭示了跨语言迁移的有效性存在模型容量阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器码到高级语言的自动反编译是逆向工程中的开放难题。尽管LLM反编译已应用于C语言，但Dart和Swift等现代语言尚属空白。研究旨在探索小型专用模型作为这些语言的惯用反编译器的可行性。

Method: 训练小型专用LLM（4B和8B），比较两种训练数据增强策略：同语言合成样本 vs 跨语言迁移（Swift→Dart）。使用CODEBLEU评估可读性，compile@k评估语法正确性。

Result: 4B专用模型达71.3 CODEBLEU（接近~480B模型的73.1）；34个自然Dart函数上compile@k5达79.4%（基线64.7%）。Swift训练数据在8B规模有效但在4B无效，提示跨语言迁移存在容量阈值。

Conclusion: 小型专用模型能以最小算力生成可读、惯用的Dart代码（含语义化标识符）。跨语言迁移的有效性受模型容量约束。

Abstract: Translating machine code into human-readable high-level languages is an open research problem in reverse engineering. Despite recent advancements in LLM-based decompilation to C, modern languages like Dart and Swift are unexplored. In this paper, we study the use of small specialized LLMs as an idiomatic decompiler for such languages. Additionally, we investigate the augmentation of training data using synthetic same-language examples, and compare it against adding human-written examples using related-language (Swift -> Dart). We apply CODEBLEU to evaluate the decompiled code readability and compile@k to measure the syntax correctness. Our experimental results show that on a 73-function Dart test dataset (representing diverse complexity levels), our 4B specialized model achieves 71.3 CODEBLEU (95% CI 65.5-77.1), approximately comparable to a ~480B code model (73.1; 67.4-78.8). On a subset of 34 natural Dart functions, it reaches compile@k5 = 79.4% (Wilson 95% CI 63.2-89.7), vs. 64.7% (47.9-78.5) for the base model; the difference is suggestive but not statistically significant at 0.05. Our results indicate that adding Swift training data helps at 8B but not at 4B, suggesting a capacity threshold for effective cross-lingual transfer. Our experimental results show that small specialized models can generate readable, idiomatic Dart with meaningful identifiers while using minimal compute.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [91] [Near-Optimal Parallel Approximate Counting via Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01263)
*David G. Harris,Vladimir Kolmogorov,Hongyang Liu,Yitong Yin,Yiyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了近似计数与采样之间的计算等价性，提出了一种简单非自适应算法和一种只需两轮适应性的算法，前者使用O(q log²h/ε²)样本，后者使用O(q logh/ε²)样本，匹配顺序算法的最优样本复杂度，并可转化为高效并行的RNC计数算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模拟退火算法虽能达到O(q logh/ε²)的最优复杂度，但本质上是顺序的或自适应的——即查询的参数β依赖于之前的样本。这限制了算法的并行化潜力。本文旨在设计非自适应或少自适应近似计数算法，以实现高效的并行计算。

Method: 1. 提出简单非自适应算法，使用O(q log²h/ε²)样本；
2. 提出两轮自适应算法，使用O(q logh/ε²)样本（匹配顺序算法最优复杂度）；
3. 基于分区函数比值Q=Z(β_max)/Z(β_min)的估计框架；
4. 算法可直接转化为RNC并行计数算法。

Result: 成功构建了非自适应和少自适应近似计数算法，在保持最优样本复杂度的同时实现了并行化。算法可应用于反铁磁2自旋模型、单体-二聚体模型和铁磁Ising模型等多种经典模型的RNC计数算法设计。

Conclusion: 本文弥合了顺序自适应算法与并行算法之间的差距，首次实现了两轮自适应下匹配最优复杂度的近似计数算法，为近似计数问题的并行化提供了理论基础和实用方法。

Abstract: The computational equivalence between approximate counting and sampling is well established for polynomial-time algorithms. The most efficient general reduction from counting to sampling is achieved via simulated annealing, where the counting problem is formulated in terms of estimating the ratio $Q={Z(β_{\max})}/{Z(β_{\min})}$ between partition functions $Z(β)=\sum_{x\in Ω} \exp(βH(x))$ of Gibbs distributions $μ_β$ over $Ω$ with Hamiltonian $H$, given access to a sampling oracle that produces samples from $μ_β$ for $β\in [β_{\min}, β_{\max}]$.
  The best bound achieved by known annealing algorithms with relative error $\varepsilon$ is $O(q \log h / \varepsilon^2)$, where $q, h$ are parameters which respectively bound $\ln Q$ and $H$. However, all known algorithms attaining this near-optimal complexity are inherently sequential, or *adaptive*: the queried parameters $β$ depend on previous samples.
  We develop a simple non-adaptive algorithm for approximate counting using $O(q \log^2 h / \varepsilon^2)$ samples, as well as an algorithm that achieves $O(q \log h / \varepsilon^2)$ samples with just two rounds of adaptivity, matching the best sample complexity of sequential algorithms. These algorithms naturally give rise to work-efficient parallel (RNC) counting algorithms.
  We discuss applications to RNC counting algorithms for several classic models, including the anti-ferromagnetic 2-spin, monomer-dimer and ferromagnetic Ising models.

</details>


### [92] [Space-Efficient Text Indexing with Mismatches using Function Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01307)
*Jackson Bibbens,Levi Borevitz,Samuel McCauley*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究在O(n)空间约束下，如何高效实现Hamming距离不超过k的近似字符串匹配查询。将Fiat-Naor函数反转技术与CGL树结合，在O(n)空间内实现了与字母表大小无关的查询时间O(|q| + log^{4k}n + log^{2k}n·#occ)，是k≥3时线性空间下的已知最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CGL树需要O(n log^k n)空间，自然问题是：在O(n)空间约束下能实现多高效的查询？此前最佳结果(Chan et al. 2011)查询时间为O(|q| + |Σ|^k log^{k^2+k}n + #occ)，对字母表大小|Σ|有不良依赖。

Method: 核心思想是将Fiat-Naor函数反转技术应用于CGL树。需重新审视Fiat-Naor数据结构和CGL树的表述以完成结合。

Result: 获得O(n)空间数据结构，查询时间约为O(|q| + log^{4k}n + log^{2k}n·#occ)，与|Σ|无关。即使|Σ|=O(1)，当k≥3且输出规模不大时，这也是线性空间下的最优查询时间。同时给出首个亚线性空间结果，使用o(n)额外空间。

Conclusion: 该工作在线性空间约束下为k≥3提供了目前最优的查询时间，且与字母表大小无关，实现了时间与空间的平滑权衡，并首次给出了次线性空间的数据结构。

Abstract: A classic data structure problem is to preprocess a string T of length $n$ so that, given a query $q$, we can quickly find all substrings of T with Hamming distance at most $k$ from the query string. Variants of this problem have seen significant research both in theory and in practice. For a wide parameter range, the best worst-case bounds are achieved by the "CGL tree" (Cole, Gottlieb, Lewenstein 2004), which achieves query time roughly $\tilde{O}(|q| + \log^k n + \# occ)$ where $\# occ$ is the size of the output, and space ${O}(n\log^k n)$. The CGL Tree space was recently improved to $O(n \log^{k-1} n)$ (Kociumaka, Radoszewski 2026).
  A natural question is whether a high space bound is necessary. How efficient can we make queries when the data structure is constrained to $O(n)$ space? While this question has seen extensive research, all known results have query time with unfavorable dependence on $n$, $k$, and the alphabet $Σ$. The state of the art query time (Chan et al. 2011) is roughly $\tilde{O}(|q| + |Σ|^k \log^{k^2 + k} n + \# occ)$.
  We give an $O(n)$-space data structure with query time roughly $\tilde{O}(|q| + \log^{4k} n + \log^{2k} n \# occ)$, with no dependence on $|Σ|$. Even if $|Σ| = O(1)$, this is the best known query time for linear space if $k\geq 3$ unless $\# occ$ is large. Our results give a smooth tradeoff between time and space. We also give the first sublinear-space results: we give a succinct data structure using only $o(n)$ space in addition to the text itself.
  Our main technical idea is to apply function inversion (Fiat, Naor 2000) to the CGL tree. Combining these techniques is not immediate; in fact, we revisit the exposition of both the Fiat-Naor data structure and the CGL tree to obtain our bounds. Along the way, we obtain improved performance for both data structures, which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [93] [A divide and conquer strategy for multinomial particle filter resampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01356)
*Andrey A. Popov*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对样本数小于等于离散分布支撑点数这一特定场景的新型多项式重采样方法，适用于高斯混合滤波器等集成混合模型滤波器，并通过计算复杂度分析和数值实验证明了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粒子滤波中的重采样步骤是计算密集型操作。当所需样本数小于等于底层离散分布的支撑点数量时（如集成混合模型滤波器中），现有标准多项式采样程序存在不必要的计算开销，需要更高效的重采样方法。

Method: 提出了一种新型多项式重采样程序，专门针对N个样本需求不超过M个支撑点的场景。方法的核心改进在于避免了标准多项式采样中对不相干随机数的计算，通过计算复杂度分析评估算法效率。

Result: 所提方法在计算复杂度上优于两种已知最优的多项式采样程序，数值实验也验证了其计算优势。

Conclusion: 该方法为粒子滤波重采样提供了一种针对特定应用场景（样本数≤支撑点数）的高效解决方案，具有重要的实用价值，特别是在需要频繁重采样的集成混合模型滤波器应用中。

Abstract: This work provides a new multinomial resampling procedure for particle filter resampling, focused on the case where the number of samples required is less than or equal to the size of the underlying discrete distribution. This setting is common in ensemble mixture model filters such as the Gaussian mixture filter. We show superiority of our approach with respect two of the best known multinomial sampling procedures both through a computational complexity analysis and through a numerical experiment.

</details>


### [94] [Approximating the Permanent of a Random Matrix with Polynomially Small Mean: Zeros and Universality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01367)
*Frederic Koehler,Pui Kuen Leung*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 提出了随机矩阵永久式（permanent）近似算法的新结果，证明了复高斯矩阵对应的随机多项式零点位于半径为~O(n^{-1/3})的圆盘内，从而在偏置为~Ω(n^{-1/3})时实现高效近似，并将先前polylog(n)级别的下界大幅降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究源于理解线性光学和boson sampling的经典计算复杂度问题。Barvinok插值方法可用于高效近似permanent，但需要多项式per(zJ+W)的零点远离零点区域（zero-free region），此前在小偏置情况下没有高效算法，且尚不清楚是否存在|z|≥1的零点。

Method: 使用Barvinok的多项式插值方法，通过分析随机多项式per(zJ+W)的零点分布区域，建立其零-free区域。核心工具包括复高斯矩阵理论、随机多项式零点分布分析，以及对硬核模型（hardcore model）在一般图上的推广。

Result: 主要结果：①当W的条目为标准复高斯分布时，per(zJ+W)的所有零点位于半径为~O(n^{-1/3})的圆盘内；②大部分零点（约(1-ε)n个）的模约为Θ(n^{-1/2})；③由此在偏置~Ω(n^{-1/3})时得到近似算法；④推广到i.i.d.亚指数分布矩阵和一般图的hardcore model。

Conclusion: 该工作将permanent近似算法的偏置要求从1/polylog(n)显著降低到~n^{-1/3}，同时证明了零点确实存在|z|≥1的情况（与之前未知形成对比）。结果不与permanent平均情况硬度的猜想矛盾，因为大部分零点的模为Θ(n^{-1/2})。

Abstract: We study algorithms for approximating the permanent of a random matrix when the entries are slightly biased away from zero. This question is motivated by the goal of understanding the classical complexity of linear optics and \emph{boson sampling} (Aaronson and Arkhipov '11; Eldar and Mehraban '17). Barvinok's interpolation method enables efficient approximation of the permanent, provided one can establish a sufficiently large zero-free region for the polynomial $\mathrm{per}(zJ + W)$, where $J$ is the all-ones matrix and $W$ is a random matrix with independent mean-zero entries.
  We show that when the entries of $W$ are standard complex Gaussians, all zeros of the random polynomial $\mathrm{per}(zJ + W)$ lie within a disk of radius $\tilde{O}(n^{-1/3})$, which yields an approximation algorithm when the bias of the entries is $\tildeΩ(n^{-1/3})$. Previously, there were no efficient algorithms at biases smaller than $1/\mathrm{polylog}(n)$, and it was unknown whether there typically exist zeros $z$ with $|z| \ge 1$. As a complementary result, we show that the bulk of the zeros, namely $(1 - ε)n$ of them, have magnitude $Θ(n^{-1/2})$. This prevents our interpolation method from contradicting the conjectured average-case hardness of approximating the permanent. We also establish analogous zero-free regions for the hardcore model on general graphs with complex vertex fugacities. In addition, we prove universality results establishing zero-free regions for random matrices $W$ with i.i.d. subexponential entries.

</details>


### [95] [A Simple Average-case Analysis of Recursive Randomized Greedy MIS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01462)
*Mina Dalirrooyfard,Konstantin Makarychev,Slobodan Mitrović*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文对随机贪心最大独立集(MIS)算法的递归版本提供了更简单的复杂度分析。证明该算法每个顶点的期望递归调用次数以上述图为界，与原始分析结果相同但更加简洁透明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yoshida等人(2009)对随机贪心MIS算法的分析虽然巧妙，但过程复杂(intricate)，难以理解和验证。需要一个更简单、更透明的分析方法来降低理论门槛。

Method: 借鉴Dalirrooyfard、Makarychev和Mitrović(2024)在相关聚类算法中开发的势函数法(potential-function-based argument)，将其思想适配到MIS问题环境中。

Result: 成功实现了与原分析相同的理论保证：算法每个顶点的期望递归调用次数以上述度为上界，同时大大简化了证明过程。

Conclusion: 通过引入势函数这一分析工具，为递归随机贪心MIS算法提供了更直接、更透明的理论分析，使算法行为更易于理解和验证。

Abstract: We revisit the complexity analysis of the recursive version of the randomized greedy algorithm for computing a maximal independent set (MIS), originally analyzed by Yoshida, Yamamoto, and Ito (2009). They showed that, on average per vertex, the expected number of recursive calls made by this algorithm is upper bounded by the average degree of the input graph. While their analysis is clever and intricate, we provide a significantly simpler alternative that achieves the same guarantee.
  Our analysis is inspired by the recent work of Dalirrooyfard, Makarychev, and Mitrović (2024), who developed a potential-function-based argument to analyze a new algorithm for correlation clustering. We adapt this approach to the MIS setting, yielding a more direct and arguably more transparent analysis of the recursive randomized greedy MIS algorithm.

</details>


### [96] [Sublinear-query relative-error testing of halfspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01557)
*Xi Chen,Anindya De,Yizhi Huang,Shivam Nadimpalli,Rocco A. Servedio,Tianqi Yang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文在布尔域半空间相对误差测试下界Ω(log n)的基础上，证明在实数域ℝ^n的高斯分布N(0, I_n)下，半空间可以用亚线性数量的oracle调用实现相对误差测试，效率显著高于学习所需的调用次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对误差性质测试模型用于测试"稀疏"布尔值函数。[CDHNSY26]证明在布尔域{0,1}^n上，半空间（线性阈值函数）的相对误差测试需要Ω(log n)次oracle调用。本文旨在为该下界提供上界补充，证明在高斯分布下可实现更高效的测试。

Method: 利用Hermite分析、高斯等周不等式以及关于噪声敏感性和表面积的几何结果，设计在标准高斯分布N(0, I_n)下对半空间进行相对误差测试的算法。

Result: 在ℝ^n的高斯分布下，半空间可以使用亚线性数量的oracle调用进行相对误差测试，显著少于学习所需的调用次数。

Conclusion: 本文结果与[CDHNSY26]的布尔域下界形成互补，表明测试域和分布类型对半空间相对误差测试的复杂度有显著影响。

Abstract: The relative-error property testing model was introduced in [CDHLNSY24] to facilitate the study of property testing for "sparse" Boolean-valued functions, i.e. ones for which only a small fraction of all input assignments satisfy the function. In this framework, the distance from the unknown target function $f$ that is being tested to a function $g$ is defined as $\mathrm{Vol}(f \mathop{\triangle} g)/\mathrm{Vol}(f)$, where the numerator is the fraction of inputs on which $f$ and $g$ disagree and the denominator is the fraction of inputs that satisfy $f$.
  Recent work [CDHNSY26] has shown that over the Boolean domain $\{0,1\}^n$, any relative-error testing algorithm for the fundamental class of halfspaces (i.e. linear threshold functions) must make $Ω(\log n)$ oracle calls. In this paper we complement the [CDHNSY26] lower bound by showing that halfspaces can be relative-error tested over $\mathbb{R}^n$ under the standard $N(0,I_n)$ Gaussian distribution using a sublinear number of oracle calls -- in particular, substantially fewer than would be required for learning. Our results use a wide range of tools including Hermite analysis, Gaussian isoperimetric inequalities, and geometric results on noise sensitivity and surface area.

</details>


### [97] [Single-Pass Streaming CSPs via Two-Tier Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01575)
*Amir Azarmehr,Soheil Behnezhad,Shane Ferrante*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文证明了在单遍流式计算模型下，对任意Max-CSP问题，利用n^(1-Ω(1))空间即可达到(α-ε)近似比，其中α是BasicLP松弛的整数间隙。这解决了该领域的一个重大猜想的第一部分，Max-DiCut作为特例也被本文算法覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式计算模型下Max-CSP问题的空间复杂度研究：已知O(n)空间可达到近乎最优的近似比，但o(n)空间下能达到的最佳近似比长期未知。领域猜想认为BasicLP的整数间隙α是关键指标——(α-ε)近似可用o(n)空间达成，而(α+ε)近似需要Ω(n)空间。

Method: 提出两层级采样（two-tier sampling）技术：结合边采样处理高degree顶点、顶点采样处理低degree顶点，在约减图上模拟合适的局部算法，实现单遍流式算法。

Result: 证明猜想第一部分：对任意固定ε>0，可利用n^(1-Ω_ε(1))空间在单遍中达到(α-ε)近似比。

Conclusion: 完全解决了猜想的"可达性"方向；通过完全不同的算法和证明恢复了ABFS26关于Max-DiCut的结果；两层级采样技术为流式CSP问题提供了新的算法框架。

Abstract: We study the maximum constraint satisfaction problem, Max-CSP, in the streaming setting. Given $n$ variables, the constraints arrive sequentially in an arbitrary order, with each constraint involving only a small subset of the variables. The objective is to approximate the maximum fraction of constraints that can be satisfied by an optimal assignment in a single pass. The problem admits a trivial near-optimal solution with $O(n)$ space, so the major open problem in the literature has been the best approximation achievable when limiting the space to $o(n)$.
  The answer to the question above depends heavily on the CSP instance at hand. The integrality gap $α$ of an LP relaxation, known as the BasicLP, plays a central role. In particular, a major conjecture of the area is that in the single-pass streaming setting, for any fixed $\varepsilon > 0$, (i) an $(α-\varepsilon)$-approximation can be achieved with $o(n)$ space, and (ii) any $(α+\varepsilon)$-approximation requires $Ω(n)$ space.
  In this work, we fully resolve the first side of the conjecture by proving that an $(α- \varepsilon)$-approximation of Max-CSP can indeed be achieved using $n^{1-Ω_\varepsilon(1)}$ space and in a single pass. Given that Max-DiCut is a special case of Max-CSP, our algorithm fully recovers the recent result of [ABFS26, STOC'26] via a completely different algorithm and proof. On a technical level, our algorithm simulates a suitable local algorithm on a reduced graph using a technique that we call *two-tier sampling*: the algorithm combines both edge sampling and vertex sampling to handle high- and low-degree vertices at the same time.

</details>


### [98] [Adaptive Fully Dynamic $k$-Center Clustering with (Near-)Optimal Worst-Case Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01726)
*Mara Grilnberger,Antonis Skarlatos*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究全动态k-center聚类问题，在对抗性点插入和删除下，同时实现常数近似比、近最优最坏情况更新时间和常数最坏情况回溯代价，并首次对自适应对抗者给出肯定答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态k-center算法无法同时满足：1）常数近似比；2）近最优更新时间；3）常数回溯代价。特别地，之前最好的结果（ICML '25）仅能对抗遗忘型对手，且只提供期望摊销保证，而非最坏情况保证。

Method: 采用两阶段方法：1）开发全动态双准则近似算法，获得（近似）最优最坏情况界；2）基于此设计全动态k-center算法。具体通过分离回溯代价和更新时间的最坏情况保证，再优雅地合并为单一算法。

Result: 对全动态k-center问题实现了：常数因子近似、近线性最坏情况更新时间、常数最坏情况回溯，即使在自适应对抗者攻击下也能保持这些保证。

Conclusion: 完满解决了动态k-center聚类中近似比、更新时间和回溯代价的协同优化问题，填补了自适应对抗者场景下的理论空白。

Abstract: Given a sequence of adversarial point insertions and point deletions, is it possible to simultaneously optimize the approximation ratio, update time, and recourse for a $k$-clustering problem? If so, can this be achieved with worst-case guarantees against an adaptive adversary? These questions have garnered significant attention in recent years.
  Prior works by Bhattacharya, Costa, Garg, Lattanzi, and Parotsidis [FOCS '24] and by Bhattacharya, Costa, and Farokhnejad [STOC '25] have taken significant steps toward this direction for the $k$-median clustering problem and its generalization, the $(k, z)$-clustering problem. In this paper, we study the $k$-center clustering problem, which is one of the most classical and well-studied $k$-clustering problems. Recently, Bhattacharya, Costa, Farokhnejad, Lattanzi, and Parotsidis [ICML '25] provided an affirmative answer to the first question for the $k$-center clustering problem. However, their work did not resolve the second question, as their result provides only expected amortized guarantees against an oblivious adversary.
  In this work, we make significant progress and close the gap by answering both questions in the affirmative. Specifically, we show that the fully dynamic $k$-center clustering problem admits a constant-factor approximation, near-optimal worst-case update time, and constant worst-case recourse, even against an adaptive adversary. This is achieved by first developing a fully dynamic bicriteria approximation algorithm with (near-)optimal worst-case bounds, and then designing a suitable fully dynamic $k$-center algorithm with near-linear update time. For the fully dynamic bicriteria approximation algorithm, we establish the worst-case recourse and worst-case update time guarantees separately, and then merge them into a single algorithm through a simple yet elegant process.

</details>


### [99] [A Constant-Approximation Distance Labeling Scheme under Polynomially Many Edge Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01829)
*Bernhard Haeupler,Yaowei Long,Antti Roeyskoe,Thatchaphol Saranurak*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 提出首个在处理任意数量边故障时仍能保持常数近似比的容错距离标记方案，标记大小为~O(f^4 n^(1/k))，近似比为O(k^4)，解决了Dory和Parter [DP21]提出的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有容错距离标记方案对于任意数量的边故障只能提供线性于f的近似比，无法在故障数量较大时保持良好的近似性能。该研究旨在突破这一限制，实现常数近似比的同时支持任意数量的边故障。

Method: 设计确定性多项式时间标记方案，将标签分配给无向带权图的顶点和边，使得仅通过读取F∪{p,q}的标签即可在移除最多f条边后近似p和q之间的距离。

Result: 获得O(k^4)近似比和~O(f^4 n^(1/k))标记大小，首次在支持任意数量边故障的条件下实现常数近似比。同时改进了距离敏感 oracle，即使对于f = Θ(log n)的故障也能克服以往方案中查询时间超线性、线性近似比或指数级近似依赖的问题。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了容错距离标记领域的核心开放问题，首次在理论上实现了常数近似与任意故障容错能力的结合，并对距离敏感 oracle 的现有技术状态进行了直接改进。

Abstract: A fault-tolerant distance labeling scheme assigns a label to each vertex and edge of an undirected weighted graph $G$ with $n$ vertices so that, for any edge set $F$ of size $|F| \leq f$, one can approximate the distance between $p$ and $q$ in $G \setminus F$ by reading only the labels of $F \cup \{p,q\}$.
  For any $k$, we present a deterministic polynomial-time scheme with $O(k^{4})$ approximation and $\tilde{O}(f^{4}n^{1/k})$ label size. This is the first scheme to achieve a constant approximation while handling any number of edge faults $f$, resolving the open problem posed by Dory and Parter [DP21]. All previous schemes provided only a linear-in-$f$ approximation [DP21, LPS25].
  Our labeling scheme directly improves the state of the art in the simpler setting of distance sensitivity oracles. Even for just $f = Θ(\log n)$ faults, all previous oracles either have super-linear query time, linear-in-$f$ approximation [CLPR12], or exponentially worse $2^{{\rm poly}(k)}$ approximation dependency in $k$ [HLS24].

</details>


### [100] [Probabilistic AVL Trees (p-AVL): Relaxing Deterministic Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02223)
*Hayagriv Desikan*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: p-AVL树是AVL树的概率变体，通过概率p决定是否修复失衡节点，实现了从BST(p=0)到标准AVL树(p=1)的连续插值。实验表明，即使很小的p值也会导致强烈的结构变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解p-AVL树族在概率参数p从0到1连续变化时的经验行为，填补BST和标准AVL树之间连续谱系的实证研究空白。

Method: 随机顺序插入实验，追踪每节点旋转次数、总失衡事件数、平均深度、平均高度和全局失衡统计量σ。

Result: 主要发现是即使很小的非零p值也已经引起强烈的结构变化，表明p-AVL树族存在一个"阈值效应"。

Conclusion: 研究目标为实证性的，旨在清晰地记录p-AVL树族的行为模式并识别主要规律，而非完整的理论证明。

Abstract: This paper studies the empirical behaviour of the p-AVL tree, a probabilistic variant of the AVL tree in which each imbalance is repaired with probability $p$. This gives an exact continuous interpolation from $p = 0$, which recovers the BST endpoint, to $p = 1$, which recovers the standard AVL tree. Across random-order insertion experiments, we track rotations per node, total imbalance events, average depth, average height, and a global imbalance statistic $σ$. The main empirical result is that even small nonzero p already causes a strong structural change. The goal here is empirical rather than fully theoretical: to document the behaviour of the p-AVL family clearly and identify the main patterns.

</details>


### [101] [Subquadratic Counting via Perfect Marginal Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02235)
*Xiaoyu Chen,Zongchen Chen,Kuikui Liu,Xinyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究自旋系统配分函数的近似计算复杂度问题。新算法突破了此前基于计数-采样归约的O(n²)时间界限，对于硬核模型等二值自旋系统，证明了完美边缘采样器的存在直接蕴含亚二次时间计数算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准计数-采样归约技术产生的算法复杂度为O(n²)（n为输入图规模），本文旨在打破这一二次时间壁垒。

Method: 提出新的计数算法框架，建立亚二次时间计数与完美边缘采样之间的深层联系。对于二值自旋系统（如硬核模型、伊辛模型），以黑盒方式将完美边缘采样器转化为亚二次计数算法；对于一般自旋系统，对现有完美边缘采样器进行适配以产生低方差边缘估计。

Result: 对于最大度Δ的图上λ加权独立集的硬核模型，当 fugacity λ < 1/(Δ-1) 时，得到O(n^(2-δ))时间近似计数算法（δ>0为常数），改进了此前的λ = o(Δ^(-3/2))结果。

Conclusion: 揭示了亚二次时间计数与完美边缘采样之间存在本质联系：完美边缘采样器的存在直接且黑盒地产生亚二次时间计数算法；该结果广泛适用于硬核模型、伊辛模型、超图独立集、顶点着色等多种自旋系统。

Abstract: We study the computational complexity of approximately computing the partition function of a spin system. Techniques based on standard counting-to-sampling reductions yield $\tilde{O}(n^2)$-time algorithms, where $n$ is the size of the input graph. We present new counting algorithms that break the quadratic-time barrier in a wide range of settings. For example, for the hardcore model of $λ$-weighted independent sets in graphs of maximum degree $Δ$, we obtain a $\tilde{O}(n^{2-δ})$-time approximate counting algorithm, for some constant $δ> 0$, when the fugacity $λ< \frac{1}{Δ-1}$, improving over the previous regime of $λ= o(Δ^{-3/2})$ by Anand, Feng, Freifeld, Guo, and Wang (2025). Our results apply broadly to many other spin systems, such as the Ising model, hypergraph independent sets, and vertex colorings.
  Interestingly, our work reveals a deep connection between $\textit{subquadratic}$ counting and $\textit{perfect}$ marginal sampling. For two-spin systems such as the hardcore and Ising models, we show that the existence of perfect marginal samplers directly yields subquadratic counting algorithms in a $\textit{black-box}$ fashion. For general spin systems, we show that almost all existing perfect marginal samplers can be adapted to produce a sufficiently low-variance marginal estimator in sublinear time, leading to subquadratic counting algorithms.

</details>

### [102] [The Digital Twin Counterfactual Framework: A Validation Architecture for Simulated Potential Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01325)
*Olav Laudy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出数字孪生反事实框架(DTCF)，通过数字孪生模拟反事实结果而非统计估计，构建从边缘保真度到结构保真度的层级假设体系，并设计五级验证架构将不可证伪的主张转化为可检验的测试，同时明确区分可验证的边际因果量（ATE、CATE、QTE）与依赖Copula的联合因果量（ITE分布、获益/危害概率、治疗效应方差）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果推断的根本问题在于反事实结果永远无法观测。现有方法（可忽略性、平行趋势、排他性限制等）均以假设替代缺失数据，无法产生真正的反事实。本文旨在突破这一范式限制，探索通过数字孪生模拟反事实的可行路径。

Method: (1) 在潜在结果框架内将数字孪生模拟器形式化为随机映射；(2) 引入边缘保真度→联合保真度→结构保真度的层级孪生假设，逐一解锁更丰富的因果量可识别类；(3) 构建五级验证架构，将不可证伪的声称转化为针对可观测数据的可检验测试；(4) 对因果量进行形式化分解，区分边际可验证量与Copula依赖量。

Result: (1) 边际因果量（ATE、CATE、QTE）可通过可观测臂比较进行检验；(2) Copula依赖量（ITE分布、获益/危害概率、治疗效应方差）永久依赖于不可观测的个体内依赖结构；(3) 建立了边界、敏感性和不确定性量化工具使Copula依赖性显式化。

Conclusion: DTCF并未解决因果推断的根本问题，但其贡献在于：边际因果主张变得可检验，联合因果主张变得显式假设索引化，两者之间的鸿沟得到形式化刻画。这为因果推断提供了一个在可检验性与可识别性之间明确权衡的框架。

Abstract: The fundamental problem of causal inference - that the counterfactual outcome for any individual is never observed - has shaped the entire methodology of the field. Every existing approach substitutes assumptions for missing data: ignorability, parallel trends, exclusion restrictions. None produces the counterfactual itself. This paper proposes the Digital Twin Counterfactual Framework (DTCF): rather than estimating the counterfactual statistically, we simulate it using a digital twin and subject the simulation to a hierarchical validation regime. We formalize the digital twin simulator as a stochastic mapping within the potential outcomes framework and introduce a hierarchy of twin fidelity assumptions - from marginal fidelity through joint fidelity to structural fidelity - each unlocking a progressively richer class of estimands. The central contribution is threefold. First, a five-level validation architecture converts the unfalsifiable claim that the simulator produces correct counterfactuals into falsifiable tests against observable data. Second, a formal decomposition separates causal quantities into those that are marginally validated (ATE, CATE, QTE - testable through observable-arm comparison) and those that are copula-dependent (the ITE distribution, probability of benefit/harm, variance of treatment effects - permanently reliant on the unobservable within-individual dependence structure). Third, bounding, sensitivity, and uncertainty quantification tools make the copula dependence explicit. The DTCF does not resolve the fundamental problem of causal inference. What it provides is a framework in which marginal causal claims become increasingly testable, joint causal claims become explicitly assumption-indexed, and the gap between the two is formally characterized.

</details>


### [103] [IDEA2: Expert-in-the-loop competency question elicitation for collaborative ontology engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01344)
*Elliott Watkiss-Leek,Reham Alharbi,Harry Rostron,Andrew Ng,Ewan Johnson,Andrew Mitchell,Terry R. Payne,Valentina Tamma,Jacopo de Berardinis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IDEA2是一个半自动化的能力问题（CQ）提取工作流，将大语言模型与专家协作相结合，通过迭代循环（LLM提取→专家反馈→LLM重写）加速本体工程中的需求工程阶段，并在科学数据和文化遗产两个实际场景中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本体工程中，能力问题（CQ）的提取是关键但资源密集的瓶颈阶段，领域专家与本体工程师之间的沟通鸿沟导致该阶段效率低下。

Method: 提出IDEA2方法，采用核心迭代循环：(1)LLM从需求文档中初始提取CQ；(2)领域专家在协作平台上进行共同审查和反馈；(3)LLM根据拒绝反馈迭代重写CQ直至达成共识。同时引入溯源模型追踪每个CQ的完整编辑谱系、匿名反馈和生成参数。

Result: 在科学数据和文化遗产两个真实场景中验证，IDEA2能够加速需求工程过程，提高CQ的接受度和相关性，并展现出较高的可用性和有效性。

Conclusion: LLM集成与专家在环的协作模式可有效弥合领域专家与本体工程师之间的沟通差距，IDEA2为半自动化CQ提取提供了可复制、透明的工作流范式。

Abstract: Competency question (CQ) elicitation represents a critical but resource-intensive bottleneck in ontology engineering. This foundational phase is often hampered by the communication gap between domain experts, who possess the necessary knowledge, and ontology engineers, who formalise it. This paper introduces IDEA2, a novel, semi-automated workflow that integrates Large Language Models (LLMs) within a collaborative, expert-in-the-loop process to address this challenge. The methodology is characterised by a core iterative loop: an initial LLM-based extraction of CQs from requirement documents, a co-creational review and feedback phase by domain experts on an accessible collaborative platform, and an iterative, feedback-driven reformulation of rejected CQs by an LLM until consensus is achieved. To ensure transparency and reproducibility, the entire lifecycle of each CQ is tracked using a provenance model that captures the full lineage of edits, anonymised feedback, and generation parameters. The workflow was validated in 2 real-world scenarios (scientific data, cultural heritage), demonstrating that IDEA2 can accelerate the requirements engineering process, improve the acceptance and relevance of the resulting CQs, and exhibit high usability and effectiveness among domain experts. We release all code and experiments at https://github.com/KE-UniLiv/IDEA2

</details>


### [104] [A Role-Based LLM Framework for Structured Information Extraction from Healthy Food Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01529)
*Congjing Zhang,Ruoxuan Bao,Jingyu Li,Yoav Ackerman,Shuai Huang,Yanfang Su*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于角色的LLM框架，用于从非结构化健康食品政策文档中自动提取信息，通过分配三个专业角色（政策分析师、法律策略专家、食品系统专家）来模拟专家分析工作流程，在608项政策上验证了相对于零样本、少样本和思维链基线方法的优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在健康食品政策领域的信息提取任务中面临幻觉、误分类和信息遗漏等问题，这些问题源于政策文档的结构多样性和不一致性，亟需一种更可靠、透明的方法来自动化政策信息提取。

Method: 提出角色化LLM框架，包含三个专业角色：LLM政策分析师（负责元数据和机制分类）、LLM法律策略专家（识别复杂法律方法）、LLM食品系统专家（分类食品系统阶段）。框架通过结构化领域知识注入角色特定提示，包括法律机制定义和分类标准。

Result: 该框架在608项HFPP数据库政策上进行了评估，使用Llama-3.3-70B模型，在复杂推理任务中展现出优于零样本、少样本和思维链基线方法的性能。

Conclusion: 基于角色的LLM框架为健康食品政策信息提取提供了一种可靠、透明的方法，能够有效处理复杂推理任务，证明了专业角色分工结合领域知识注入在提升LLM信息提取准确性方面的有效性。

Abstract: Current Large Language Model (LLM) approaches for information extraction (IE) in the healthy food policy domain are often hindered by various factors, including misinformation, specifically hallucinations, misclassifications, and omissions that result from the structural diversity and inconsistency of policy documents. To address these limitations, this study proposes a role-based LLM framework that automates the IE from unstructured policy data by assigning specialized roles: an LLM policy analyst for metadata and mechanism classification, an LLM legal strategy specialist for identifying complex legal approaches, and an LLM food system expert for categorizing food system stages. This framework mimics expert analysis workflows by incorporating structured domain knowledge, including explicit definitions of legal mechanisms and classification criteria, into role-specific prompts. We evaluate the framework using 608 healthy food policies from the Healthy Food Policy Project (HFPP) database, comparing its performance against zero-shot, few-shot, and chain-of-thought (CoT) baselines using Llama-3.3-70B. Our proposed framework demonstrates superior performance in complex reasoning tasks, offering a reliable and transparent methodology for automating IE from health policies.

</details>


### [105] [Semantic Modeling for World-Centered Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01359)
*Andrei Mantsivoda,Darya Gavrilina*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We introduce world-centered multi-agent systems (WMAS) as an alternative to traditional agent-centered architectures, arguing that structured domains such as enterprises and institutional systems require a shared, explicit world representation to ensure semantic consistency, explainability, and long-term stability. We classify worlds along dimensions including ontological explicitness, normativity, etc. In WMAS, learning and coordination operate over a shared world model rather than isolated agent-local representations, enabling global consistency and verifiable system behavior. We propose semantic models as a mathematical formalism for representing such worlds. Finally, we present the Ontobox platform as a realization of WMAS.

</details>


### [106] [Crashing Waves vs. Rising Tides: Preliminary Findings on AI Automation from Thousands of Worker Evaluations of Labor Market Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01363)
*Matthias Mertens,Adam Kuzee,Brittany S. Harris,Harry Lyu,Wensu Li,Jonathan Rosenfeld,Meiri Anto,Martin Fleming,Neil Thompson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI自动化是一个连续谱：一端是"冲击波"（在特定任务上的突然能力跃升），另一端是"涨潮"（持续、广泛的进步）。通过对美国劳工部O*NET数据库中3000多项基于文本的任务进行超过17000次评估，研究发现AI自动化的主要形式是"涨潮"而非"冲击波"。按当前发展趋势，预计到2029年AI可在大多数文本相关任务上达到80-95%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究（如METR）发现了AI能力的"冲击波"现象（特定任务上的突然能力跃升），但本文认为需要验证这一发现是否具有普遍性，以及AI自动化是否主要以连续、广泛的形式发展。这对于理解AI对经济和社会的影响至关重要。

Method: 基于美国劳工部O*NET数据库，选取3000多项文本类任务，由来自这些岗位的工人进行超过17000次AI能力评估。研究采用任务完成率作为衡量指标。

Result: （1）未发现"冲击波"的明显证据；（2）"涨潮"是AI自动化的主要形式——AI在广泛任务上表现优异且快速提升；（3）2024年Q2：完成人类需3-4小时的任务成功率为~50%，2025年Q3升至~65%；（4）若趋势持续，2029年可达80-95%成功率（最低质量标准）。

Conclusion: AI能力正以持续、广泛的方式提升，而非在特定任务上的突变。文本处理任务的自动化将逐步但全面地推进，而经济和劳动力市场的影响需要更长时间才能显现。

Abstract: We propose that AI automation is a continuum between: (i) crashing waves where AI capabilities surge abruptly over small sets of tasks, and (ii) rising tides where the increase in AI capabilities is more continuous and broad-based. We test for these effects in preliminary evidence from an ongoing evaluation of AI capabilities across over 3,000 broad-based tasks derived from the U.S. Department of Labor O*NET categorization that are text-based and thus LLM-addressable. Based on more than 17,000 evaluations by workers from these jobs, we find little evidence of crashing waves (in contrast to recent work by METR), but substantial evidence that rising tides are the primary form of AI automation. AI performance is high and improving rapidly across a wide range of tasks. We estimate that, in 2024-Q2, AI models successfully complete tasks that take humans approximately 3-4 hours with about a 50% success rate, increasing to about 65% by 2025-Q3. If recent trends in AI capability growth persist, this pace of AI improvement implies that LLMs will be able to complete most text-related tasks with success rates of, on average, 80%-95% by 2029 at a minimally sufficient quality level. Achieving near-perfect success rates at this quality level or comparable success rates at superior quality would require several additional years. These AI capability improvements would impact the economy and labor market as organizations adopt AI, which could have a substantially longer timeline.

</details>


### [107] [CogBias: Measuring and Mitigating Cognitive Bias in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01366)
*Fan Huang,Songheng Zhang,Haewoon Kwak,Jisun An*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM认知偏差基准LLM CogBias，通过激活空间线性探测和激活 steering 技术，系统性地识别并缓解大语言模型中的认知偏差，同时保持下游任务能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明LLM表现出认知偏差行为，但这些偏差是否对应可识别的内部表征，以及能否通过针对性干预加以缓解，仍是开放问题。

Method: 1) 定义LLM认知偏差为在具有可计算ground-truth的任务中系统性的、可复现的答案偏离；2) 构建LLM CogBias基准，涵盖四类认知偏差：判断偏差、信息处理偏差、社会偏差和响应偏差；3) 使用对比设计下的线性探测方法验证偏差在激活空间中是否线性可分；4) 应用激活 steering 技术调节偏差行为。

Result: 1) 三种LLM在四类偏差上均呈现系统性偏差，但幅度和去偏效果高度依赖于偏差家族：prompt级去偏对响应偏差效果显著，但会加剧判断偏差；2) 偏差在模型激活空间中表现为线性可分方向；3) 激活 steering 可降低26-32%的偏差分数，同时在25个下游基准上保持能力（Llama几乎无退化；Qwen在判断偏差上最多下降19.0pp）；4) 尽管不同模型间偏差表征近乎正交（平均余弦相似度0.01），steering在不同架构上的偏差降低率高度相关（r(246)=.621, p<.001），暗示存在共享的功能组织结构。

Conclusion: LLM认知偏差可通过激活空间中的线性表征有效识别和干预，且不同模型架构可能遵循相似的偏差功能组织原则。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in high-stakes decision-making contexts. While prior work has shown that LLMs exhibit cognitive biases behaviorally, whether these biases correspond to identifiable internal representations and can be mitigated through targeted intervention remains an open question. We define LLM cognitive bias as systematic, reproducible deviations from correct answers in tasks with computable ground-truth baselines, and introduce LLM CogBias, a benchmark organized around four families of cognitive biases: Judgment, Information Processing, Social, and Response. We evaluate three LLMs and find that cognitive biases emerge systematically across all four families, with magnitudes and debiasing responses that are strongly family-dependent: prompt-level debiasing substantially reduces Response biases but backfires for Judgment biases. Using linear probes under a contrastive design, we show that these biases are encoded as linearly separable directions in model activation space. Finally, we apply activation steering to modulate biased behavior, achieving 26--32\% reduction in bias score (fraction of biased responses) while preserving downstream capability on 25 benchmarks (Llama: negligible degradation; Qwen: up to $-$19.0pp for Judgment biases). Despite near-orthogonal bias representations across models (mean cosine similarity 0.01), steering reduces bias at similar rates across architectures ($r(246)$=.621, $p$<.001), suggesting shared functional organization.

</details>


### [108] [RIFT: A RubrIc Failure Mode Taxonomy and Automated Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01375)
*Zhengyang Qi,Charles Dickens,Derek Pham,Amanda Dsouza,Armin Parchami,Frederic Sala,Paroma Varma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RIFT（RubrIc Failure mode Taxonomy），一个用于系统表征LLM评估中评分规则（rubric）失效模式的分类体系。该体系包含8种失效模式，分为三大类：可靠性失效、内容效度失效和结果效度失效。通过对5个不同基准测试（涵盖通用指令遵循、代码生成、创意写作和专家级深度研究）的评分规则进行迭代标注开发而成。最终提出自动化评分规则质量指标，与人工标注对齐达到0.86 F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型基准测试和训练流程中，基于评分规则的评估被广泛使用，但目前无法仅从聚合或下游信号中诊断评分规则的质量问题。

Method: 采用扎根理论方法，通过迭代标注开发分类体系；对5个不同基准测试中的评分规则进行标注，直至无新失效模式出现；通过独立人工标注者一致性评估分类体系一致性；提出自动化评分规则质量指标。

Result: RIFT包含8种失效模式，分属3个大类；人工标注者间一致性达87%成对一致率和0.64平均Cohen's kappa；自动化指标与人工失效模式标注对齐，F1分数达0.86。

Conclusion: RIFT提供了系统性诊断LLM评估中评分规则质量问题的框架，支持可扩展的自动化质量评估。

Abstract: Rubric-based evaluation is widely used in LLM benchmarks and training pipelines for open-ended, less verifiable tasks. While prior work has demonstrated the effectiveness of rubrics using downstream signals such as reinforcement learning outcomes, there remains no principled way to diagnose rubric quality issues from such aggregated or downstream signals alone. To address this gap, we introduce RIFT: RubrIc Failure mode Taxonomy, a taxonomy for systematically characterizing failure modes in rubric composition and design. RIFT consists of eight failure modes organized into three high-level categories: Reliability Failures, Content Validity Failures, and Consequential Validity Failures. RIFT is developed using grounded theory by iteratively annotating rubrics drawn from five diverse benchmarks spanning general instruction following, code generation, creative writing, and expert-level deep research, until no new failure modes are identified. We evaluate the consistency of the taxonomy by measuring agreement among independent human annotators, observing fair agreement overall (87% pairwise agreement and 0.64 average Cohen's kappa). Finally, to support scalable diagnosis, we propose automated rubric quality metrics and show that they align with human failure-mode annotations, achieving up to 0.86 F1.

</details>


### [109] [Leveraging the Value of Information in POMDP Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01434)
*Zakariya Laouar,Qi Heng Ho,Zachary Sunberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VOIMCP算法，通过利用价值信息(VOI)在信念空间中的变化结构，在VOI低时选择性忽略观察信息以减少计算开销，在有限规划时间内获得更高效的POMDP策略。理论证明了其近优性并提供非渐近收敛界，基准测试表现优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: POMDP是处理状态和转移不确定性的重要形式化框架，但在有限规划时间内获得高性能策略仍面临重大挑战，主要受"维度诅咒"和"历史诅咒"的制约。

Method: 1) 提出动态规划框架，根据信念空间中VOI的分布条件性处理观察；2) 在此基础上设计VOIMCP算法，通过在VOI低时选择性忽略观察信息来避免不必要的观察分支；3) 提供理论分析保证近优性和非渐近收敛界。

Result: VOIMCP通过选择性信息处理更高效分配计算资源，仿真评估在多个POMDP基准问题上优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 利用VOI空间异质性结构是克服POMDP计算挑战的有效途径，VOIMCP在性能和效率之间取得了更好的平衡。

Abstract: Partially observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs) offer a principled formalism for planning under state and transition uncertainty. Despite advances made towards solving large POMDPs, obtaining performant policies under limited planning time remains a major challenge due to the curse of dimensionality and the curse of history.
