Method: 提出STABLE框架，包含三个核心组件：1) AUTO（增强异构语义感知）度量，用于联合测量特征相似性和属性一致性；2) HELP（异构语义关系图）索引，基于AUTO构建以组织异构语义关系；3) 动态异构路由方法，实现高效搜索。

Result: 在五个具有不同属性基数的特征向量基准数据集上进行广泛实验，结果表明STABLE在准确率、效率和鲁棒性方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: STABLE框架能够在大规模异构数据场景下实现准确、高效且鲁棒的混合ANNS，有效解决了数据分布异构性带来的挑战。

Abstract: Hybrid Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search (Hybrid ANNS) is a foundational search technology for large-scale heterogeneous data and has gained significant attention in both academia and industry. However, current approaches overlook the heterogeneity in data distribution, thus ignoring two major challenges: the Compatibility Barrier for Similarity Magnitude Heterogeneity and the Tolerance Bottleneck to Attribute Cardinality. To overcome these issues, we propose the robuSt heTerogeneity-Aware hyBrid retrievaL framEwork, STABLE, designed for accurate, efficient, and robust hybrid ANNS under datasets with various distributions. Specifically, we introduce an enhAnced heterogeneoUs semanTic perceptiOn (AUTO) metric to achieve a joint measurement of feature similarity and attribute consistency, addressing similarity magnitude heterogeneity and improving robustness to datasets with various attribute cardinalities. Thereafter, we construct our Heterogeneous sEmantic reLation graPh (HELP) index based on AUTO to organize heterogeneous semantic relations. Finally, we employ a novel Dynamic Heterogeneity Routing method to ensure an efficient search. Extensive experiments on five feature vector benchmarks with various attribute cardinalities demonstrate the superior performance of STABLE.

</details>


### [218] [From BM25 to Corrective RAG: Benchmarking Retrieval Strategies for Text-and-Table Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01733)
*Meftun Akarsu,Recep Kaan Karaman,Christopher Mierbach*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对RAG系统中的检索方法进行了系统性基准测试，针对包含文本和表格异构内容的金融问答场景。实验涵盖10种检索策略，结果表明混合检索+神经重排序的两阶段pipeline表现最佳，同时挑战了语义搜索优于BM25的常见假设，并发现查询扩展对精确数值查询效果有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RAG系统严重依赖检索质量，但针对异构文档（文本+表格混合）的现代检索方法缺乏系统性对比研究，这限制了实际应用中检索策略的选择与优化。

Method: 在金融QA基准（23,088条查询，7,318份文本-表格混合文档）上，评测10种检索策略（稀疏检索、稠密检索、混合融合、交叉编码器重排序、查询扩展、索引增强、自适应检索），采用Recall@k、MRR、nDCG和端到端Number Match指标，并进行配对Bootstrap显著性检验。

Result: 两阶段混合检索+神经重排序pipeline达Recall@5=0.816、MRR@3=0.605，显著优于单阶段方法；BM25在金融文档上优于最先进的稠密检索方法；HyDE、多查询等查询扩展及自适应检索对精确数值查询收益有限，而上下文检索则持续有效。

Conclusion: 混合检索与神经重排序的组合是提升RAG检索质量的有效范式；在特定领域（如金融），传统BM25可能仍优于语义搜索；检索策略需根据查询类型（数值型vs语义型）进行差异化设计，而非采用通用方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems critically depend on retrieval quality, yet no systematic comparison of modern retrieval methods exists for heterogeneous documents containing both text and tabular data. We benchmark ten retrieval strategies spanning sparse, dense, hybrid fusion, cross-encoder reranking, query expansion, index augmentation, and adaptive retrieval on a challenging financial QA benchmark of 23,088 queries over 7,318 documents with mixed text-and-table content. We evaluate retrieval quality via Recall@k, MRR, and nDCG, and end-to-end generation quality via Number Match, with paired bootstrap significance testing. Our results show that (1) a two-stage pipeline combining hybrid retrieval with neural reranking achieves Recall@5 of 0.816 and MRR@3 of 0.605, outperforming all single-stage methods by a large margin; (2) BM25 outperforms state-of-the-art dense retrieval on financial documents, challenging the common assumption that semantic search universally dominates; and (3) query expansion methods (HyDE, multi-query) and adaptive retrieval provide limited benefit for precise numerical queries, while contextual retrieval yields consistent gains. We provide ablation studies on fusion methods and reranker depth, actionable cost-accuracy recommendations, and release our full benchmark code.

</details>


### [219] [Do We Need Bigger Models for Science? Task-Aware Retrieval with Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01965)
*Florian Kelber,Matthias Jobst,Yuni Susanti,Michael Färber*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究探讨了在科学应用场景中，小型语言模型配合精心设计的检索管道是否能够替代大型模型。研究设计了轻量级检索增强框架，包含任务感知路由机制，整合全文科学文献和结构化元数据，并使用紧凑的指令微调模型生成带引用的回答。实验表明检索设计与模型规模是互补关系，检索设计可部分补偿小模型不足，但对复杂推理任务模型能力仍很重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学知识发现系统依赖数百亿参数规模的专有模型，限制了研究的可重复性和可及性。研究者提出核心问题：在科学应用中是否真的需要更大的模型？

Method: 设计轻量级检索增强框架，包含三个关键组件：(1) 任务感知路由机制，根据输入查询选择专门的检索策略；(2) 整合全文科学论文和结构化学术元数据；(3) 使用紧凑的指令微调语言模型生成带引用的回答。评估涵盖学术问答（单文档和多文档场景）、生物医学领域迁移问答及科学文本压缩等任务。

Result: 检索和模型规模是互补而非可替代的关系。精心设计的检索管道可以部分补偿小型模型的不足，但模型容量对于复杂推理任务仍然至关重要。

Conclusion: 检索设计和任务感知设计是构建实用、可重复的科学助手的关键因素。该研究为使用更少参数实现高效科学知识发现提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Scientific knowledge discovery increasingly relies on large language models, yet many existing scholarly assistants depend on proprietary systems with tens or hundreds of billions of parameters. Such reliance limits reproducibility and accessibility for the research community. In this work, we ask a simple question: do we need bigger models for scientific applications? Specifically, we investigate to what extent carefully designed retrieval pipelines can compensate for reduced model scale in scientific applications. We design a lightweight retrieval-augmented framework that performs task-aware routing to select specialized retrieval strategies based on the input query. The system further integrates evidence from full-text scientific papers and structured scholarly metadata, and employs compact instruction-tuned language models to generate responses with citations. We evaluate the framework across several scholarly tasks, focusing on scholarly question answering (QA), including single- and multi-document scenarios, as well as biomedical QA under domain shift and scientific text compression. Our findings demonstrate that retrieval and model scale are complementary rather than interchangeable. While retrieval design can partially compensate for smaller models, model capacity remains important for complex reasoning tasks. This work highlights retrieval and task-aware design as key factors for building practical and reproducible scholarly assistants.

</details>


### [220] [Multi-Agent Video Recommenders: Evolution, Patterns, and Open Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02211)
*Srivaths Ranganathan,Abhishek Dharmaratnakar,Anushree Sinha,Debanshu Das*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于多智能体视频推荐系统（MAVRS）的综述论文，涵盖从传统单模型推荐系统到基于大语言模型（LLM）的多智能体架构的演进历程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单模型推荐系统优化静态参与度指标，难以满足现代平台动态需求；多智能体架构通过协调专业化智能体（视频理解、推理、记忆、反馈等）提供更精准、可解释的推荐服务。

Method: 提出MAVRS分类体系，分析跨视频领域（短视频至教育平台）的协作模式与协调机制；综述代表性框架，包括早期MARL系统（如MMRF）和近期LLM驱动架构（如MACRec、Agent4Rec）。

Result: 系统梳理了多智能体视频推荐系统的演化路径，整合了多智能体推荐系统、基础模型和对话AI的核心理念。

Conclusion: 指出可扩展性、多模态理解、激励对齐等开放挑战；识别混合RL-LLM系统、终身个性化、自进化推荐系统等研究方向。

Abstract: Video recommender systems are among the most popular and impactful applications of AI, shaping content consumption and influencing culture for billions of users. Traditional single-model recommenders, which optimize static engagement metrics, are increasingly limited in addressing the dynamic requirements of modern platforms. In response, multi-agent architectures are redefining how video recommender systems serve, learn, and adapt to both users and datasets. These agent-based systems coordinate specialized agents responsible for video understanding, reasoning, memory, and feedback, to provide precise, explainable recommendations. In this survey, we trace the evolution of multi-agent video recommendation systems (MAVRS). We combine ideas from multi-agent recommender systems, foundation models, and conversational AI, culminating in the emerging field of large language model (LLM)-powered MAVRS. We present a taxonomy of collaborative patterns and analyze coordination mechanisms across diverse video domains, ranging from short-form clips to educational platforms. We discuss representative frameworks, including early multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) systems such as MMRF and recent LLM-driven architectures like MACRec and Agent4Rec, to illustrate these patterns. We also outline open challenges in scalability, multimodal understanding, incentive alignment, and identify research directions such as hybrid reinforcement learning-LLM systems, lifelong personalization and self-improving recommender systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [221] [DAXFS: A Lock-Free Shared Filesystem for CXL Disaggregated Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01620)
*Cong Wang,Yiwei Yang,Yusheng Zheng*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: DaxFS是首个利用CXL共享内存实现无锁多主机协调的Linux文件系统，通过cmpxchg原子操作实现分布式锁机制，结合CAS哈希覆盖和MH-clock页面置换算法，在CXL 3.0模拟环境中验证了>99%的CAS准确率和优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CXL技术虽支持多主机共享字节寻址内存并提供硬件级缓存一致性，但现有文件系统均未利用此特性实现无锁多主机协调。

Method: DaxFS采用cmpxchg原子操作作为唯一协调原语，利用CXL的跨主机一致性实现无锁并发写入；设计CAS-based哈希覆盖层实现多主机无锁写入；提出MH-clock多主机时钟置换算法实现协作式共享页缓存。

Result: QEMU模拟CXL 3.0环境下，双虚拟主机共享内存测试中交叉竞争时保持>99% CAS准确率无丢失更新；单主机DRAM DAX性能超越tmpfs，4线程随机写吞吐量提升2.68倍，64KB随机读吞吐量提升1.18倍；GPU微基准测试表明cmpxchg设计可扩展至GPU线程并达到PCIe 5.0带宽极限。

Conclusion: 基于cmpxchg的设计可有效扩展至多主机协调场景，实现无锁高效的文件系统操作，为CXL共享内存环境下的存储系统提供可行方案。

Abstract: CXL (Compute Express Link) enables multiple hosts to share byte-addressable memory with hardware cache coherence, but no existing filesystem exploits this for lock-free multi-host coordination. We present DaxFS, a Linux filesystem for CXL shared memory that uses cmpxchg atomic operations, which CXL makes coherent across host boundaries, as its sole coordination primitive. A CAS-based hash overlay enables lock-free concurrent writes from multiple hosts without any centralized coordinator. A cooperative shared page cache with a novel multi-host clock eviction algorithm (MH-clock) provides demand-paged caching in shared DAX memory, with fully decentralized victim selection via cmpxchg. We validate multi-host correctness using QEMU-emulated CXL 3.0, where two virtual hosts share a memory region with TCP-forwarded atomics. Under cross-host contention, DaxFS maintains >99% CAS accuracy with no lost updates. On single-host DRAM-backed DAX, DaxFS exceeds tmpfs throughput across all write workloads, achieving up to 2.68x higher random write throughput with 4 threads and 1.18x higher random read throughput at 64 KB. Preliminary GPU microbenchmarks show that the cmpxchg-based design extends to GPU threads performing page cache operations at PCIe 5.0 bandwidth limits.

</details>


### [222] [HACache: Leveraging Read Performance with Cache in a Heterogeneous Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01655)
*Jialin Liu,Liang Shi,Dingcui Yu*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 针对RAID阵列中SSD异构性问题，HACache引入高性能SSD作为读缓存，通过请求分流和缓存容量动态调节来平衡负载，提升异构RAID读性能约35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成本敏感部署中，SSD因老化或替换导致性能异构，传统条带化均匀分配请求使慢速SSD成为瓶颈，快速SSD利用不足，整体带宽受限于最慢设备。

Method: (1) 形式化定义请求分流问题并求最优解；(2) 双阶段请求分流比调整机制支持运行时优化；(3) 基于命中率和分流需求的缓存容量动态调节。

Result: 实验表明HACache显著提升异构RAID读性能，典型混合配置下带宽增益约35%。

Conclusion: 通过缓存重构请求分布结合自适应调节机制，可有效解决SSD异构性带来的性能瓶颈问题。

Abstract: In cost-sensitive deployments, RAID arrays may combine SSDs with different performance levels. Such heterogeneity arises when aging SSDs degrade yet remain usable, or when failed drives are replaced with new devices of explicitly better performance. While this reduces procurement cost, it creates performance challenges: traditional striping mecahnism distributes requests evenly, but slower SSDs become bottlenecks, leaving faster ones underutilized and limiting overall bandwidth to the slowest drive.
  To address this, we propose HACache (Heterogeneity Adaptive Cache) for read-intensive workloads. HACache introduces high-performance SSDs as read caches to rebalance request distribution. First, we formalize the request diversion problem and solve it formally. Second, to support optimal diversion ratios searching at runtime, HACache adopts a two-phase request diversion ratio adjustment mechanism. Finally, a cache capacity regulation is adopted to adapt quotas for each backend SSD based on hit rates and request diversion needs. This design maximizes bandwidth utilization. Experiments show HACache improves heterogeneous RAID read performance significantly, with bandwidth gains of about 35\% in typical mixed configurations.

</details>
